// Monday-anchored week key, so a prep cooked Sunday night belongs to the week
// it actually gets eaten in.
function weekStartOf(dateStr) {
  const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split("-").map(Number);
  const dt = new Date(y, m - 1, d);
  const dow = (dt.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  dt.setDate(dt.getDate() - dow);
  return localDateStr(dt);
}

function weekDays(weekStart) {
  const [y, m, d] = weekStart.split("-").map(Number);
  return Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => {
    const dt = new Date(y, m - 1, d + i);
    return { date: localDateStr(dt), label: ["M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S", "S"][i] };
  });
}

// Make-ahead lunches get cooked on Sunday for the week that is about to start.
// weekStartOf() puts Sunday in the week that is *ending*, so a Sunday prep landed
// in the outgoing week and read as "last week" by Monday morning - backwards for
// the one cooking pattern this tab exists to support.
function prepWeekOf(dateStr) {
  const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split("-").map(Number);
  const dt = new Date(y, m - 1, d);
  if (dt.getDay() === 0) {
    dt.setDate(dt.getDate() + 1);
    return localDateStr(dt);
  }
  return weekStartOf(dateStr);
}

function recipeForWeek(mealPlan, dateStr) {
  const wk = prepWeekOf(dateStr);
  const hit = (mealPlan || []).find((m) => m.weekStart === wk);
  return hit ? hit.recipeKey : null;
}

// The list gets read while walking a store, so it is grouped the way the store
// is laid out and names things the way the shelf tag does. Older recipes stored
// a flat fresh/pantry split; those still render, just under two headings.
const STORE_SECTIONS = [
  ["produce", "PRODUCE"],
  ["meatSeafood", "MEAT & SEAFOOD"],
  ["deli", "DELI & PREPARED"],
  ["dairy", "DAIRY & EGGS"],
  ["bakery", "BAKERY"],
  ["cannedJarred", "CANNED & JARRED"],
  ["dryGoods", "DRY GOODS & GRAINS"],
  ["spices", "SPICES & OILS"],
  ["frozen", "FROZEN"],
  ["other", "OTHER"],
];

// The chef sometimes returns departments as empty arrays or arrays holding a
// single blank string; both would render as an empty heading on the list.
function normalizeGrocery(g) {
  const src = g || {};
  const out = {};
  STORE_SECTIONS.forEach(([k]) => {
    const items = Array.isArray(src[k]) ? src[k].map((s) => String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
    if (items.length) out[k] = items;
  });
  ["fresh", "pantry"].forEach((k) => {
    const items = Array.isArray(src[k]) ? src[k].map((s) => String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
    if (items.length) out[k] = items;
  });
  if (src.notes && String(src.notes).trim()) out.notes = String(src.notes).trim();
  return out;
}

function buildGroceryList(recipe) {
  const g = normalizeGrocery(recipe.grocery);
  const line = (x) => "- " + x;
  const out = [recipe.name];
  if (recipe.servings) out.push(`${recipe.servings} serving${recipe.servings === 1 ? "" : "s"}`);
  out.push("");

  const sectioned = STORE_SECTIONS.filter(([k]) => Array.isArray(g[k]) && g[k].length);
  if (sectioned.length) {
    sectioned.forEach(([k, label]) => {
      out.push(label, ...g[k].map(line), "");
    });
  } else {
    if ((g.fresh || []).length) out.push("FRESH", ...g.fresh.map(line), "");
    if ((g.pantry || []).length) out.push("PANTRY", ...g.pantry.map(line), "");
  }
  if (g.notes) out.push("NOTES", g.notes);
  return out.join("\n").trim();
}

function hasGrocery(recipe) {
  const g = normalizeGrocery(recipe.grocery);
  return Object.keys(g).some((k) => k !== "notes");
}

// ---------- session time budget ----------
// A program can only grow until it stops fitting in the time available.
// Past that point a trainer swaps movements rather than adding them, so the
// expansion engine needs to know how long a session already runs.
const TIME_WARMUP = 3;
const TIME_SAUNA = 15;
const TIME_OVERHEAD = 10; // changing, walking between stations, waiting for equipment

function estimateSessionMinutes(exercises, allStretches, sessionType, cardioMinutes, sessions = []) {
  const lifting = Object.keys(exercises)
    .filter((k) => {
      const cfg = exercises[k];
      if (cfg.active === false) return false;
      if (!cfg.sessionType || cfg.sessionType === "both") return true;
      return cfg.sessionType === sessionType;
    })
    .reduce((sum, k) => sum + (exercises[k].estMinutes || 6), 0);

  const stretchSeconds = selectSessionStretches(sessionType, allStretches, sessions).reduce((sum, cfg) => {
    const rounds = cfg.baseRounds || 1;
    return sum + stretchAccum(cfg, rounds) * (cfg.perSide ? 2 : 1);
  }, 0);

  return Math.round(
    (cardioMinutes || 30) + TIME_WARMUP + lifting + stretchSeconds / 60 + 2 + TIME_SAUNA + TIME_OVERHEAD
  );
}

// ---------- program expansion engine ----------
// This is the part that acts like a trainer building a program out over time:
// find which movement patterns aren't trained at all, check whether the
// foundation for the missing one is solid yet, and name one specific thing
// to add. One at a time, with a settling period — adding several movements
// at once is how people end up sore, inconsistent, and quitting.

const SETTLING_SESSIONS = 3;

function coveredPatterns(exercises) {
  const covered = new Set();
  Object.values(exercises).forEach((cfg) => {
    if (cfg.active === false) return;
    (cfg.patterns || []).forEach((p) => covered.add(p));
  });
  return covered;
}

// An exercise counts as "established" once it's actually moving: either the
// weight has gone up since it started, or it's been rated solid/easy for
// several sessions. That's the signal there's room to take on something new.
function isEstablished(sessions, key) {
  const history = sessions
    .filter((s) => s.exercises && s.exercises[key])
    .map((s) => s.exercises[key]);
  if (history.length < 3) return false;
  const recent = history.slice(-3);
  if (recent.some((h) => h.feel === "struggled")) return false;
  const first = history[0];
  const last = history[history.length - 1];
  const weightUp = (last.weight ?? 0) > (first.weight ?? 0);
  const repsUp = (last.reps ?? 0) > (first.reps ?? 0);
  const steadySolid = recent.every((h) => h.feel === "easy" || h.feel === "solid");
  return weightUp || repsUp || steadySolid;
}

// How many sessions ago the newest active exercise first showed up. Used to
// enforce a settling period so additions don't stack up.
function sessionsSinceLastAddition(sessions, exercises) {
  const withEx = sessions.filter((s) => s.exercises && Object.keys(s.exercises).length > 0);
  if (withEx.length === 0) return Infinity;

  // Only movements added from the library carry addedAt. Base exercises are
  // exempt, so a rarely-logged default never blocks expansion forever.
  const added = Object.keys(exercises).filter((k) => exercises[k].active !== false && exercises[k].addedAt);
  if (added.length === 0) return Infinity;

  let fewest = Infinity;
  added.forEach((key) => {
    const since = withEx.filter((s) => s.date >= exercises[key].addedAt).length;
    // Sessions logged since it was added — if it was added today, that's 0
    // and the settling period holds until it's actually been trained.
    if (since < fewest) fewest = since;
  });
  return fewest;
}

function suggestExerciseExpansion(sessions, exercises, allStretches = STRETCHES, sessionType = "A", budgetMinutes = 120, cardioMinutes = 30) {
  const withEx = sessions.filter((s) => s.exercises && Object.keys(s.exercises).length > 0);
  if (withEx.length < 4) {
    return { ready: false, reason: `Log ${4 - withEx.length} more session${4 - withEx.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} before adding new movements — the app needs a baseline first.` };
  }

  const settling = sessionsSinceLastAddition(sessions, exercises);
  if (settling < SETTLING_SESSIONS) {
    return { ready: false, reason: `Something new was added recently. Give it ${SETTLING_SESSIONS - settling} more session${SETTLING_SESSIONS - settling === 1 ? "" : "s"} to settle before adding anything else.` };
  }

  const covered = coveredPatterns(exercises);
  const candidates = EXERCISE_LIBRARY
    .filter((c) => !exercises[c.key] || exercises[c.key].active === false)
    .filter((c) => (c.patterns || []).some((p) => !covered.has(p)))
    .sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    return { ready: false, reason: "Every major movement pattern is covered. Keep progressing what you have — there's no gap to fill right now." };
  }

  const next = candidates[0];

  // Time check comes before the prerequisite check: if there's no room in the
  // session, it doesn't matter whether the foundation is ready.
  const currentMinutes = estimateSessionMinutes(exercises, allStretches, next.sessionType, cardioMinutes, sessions);
  if (currentMinutes + (next.estMinutes || 6) > budgetMinutes) {
    return {
      ready: false,
      atCapacity: true,
      reason: `Session ${next.sessionType} already runs about ${currentMinutes} min, and adding ${next.label} would push past your ${budgetMinutes}-minute limit. From here, growth means swapping a movement out rather than adding one — or trimming cardio or sauna time to make room.`,
      pending: next,
    };
  }

  if (next.prereq && !isEstablished(sessions, next.prereq)) {
    const prereqLabel = exercises[next.prereq]?.label || EXERCISE_LIBRARY.find((e) => e.key === next.prereq)?.label || next.prereq;
    return {
      ready: false,
      reason: `${next.label} is the next gap to fill, but ${prereqLabel} should be progressing steadily first. Build that up and this will unlock.`,
      pending: next,
    };
  }

  const gap = (next.patterns || []).find((p) => !covered.has(p));
  return {
    ready: true,
    suggestion: next,
    gapLabel: PATTERNS[gap] || gap,
    reason: next.why,
  };
}

function suggestStretchExpansion(sessions, activeStretches) {
  const sessionCount = sessions.filter((s) => s.stretchRounds && Object.keys(s.stretchRounds).length > 0).length;
  if (sessionCount < 4) {
    return { ready: false, reason: `Log ${4 - sessionCount} more session${4 - sessionCount === 1 ? "" : "s"} with stretches before expanding the routine.` };
  }

  const activeKeys = new Set(activeStretches.map((s) => s.key));
  const candidates = STRETCH_LIBRARY
    .filter((c) => !activeKeys.has(c.key))
    .sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    return { ready: false, reason: "The stretch routine covers every major area. Keep it consistent rather than adding more." };
  }

  // Only expand the stretch routine once the current one is actually being
  // done — adding movements to a routine that's being skipped just makes it
  // longer and easier to skip.
  // Measured against the stretches that session actually offered, not against
  // the whole pool. Once sessions are capped, the pool is deliberately larger
  // than any one session, so scoring against it would read a fully completed
  // routine as half-finished and freeze the pool at its current size forever.
  const recent = sessions.slice(-3).filter((s) => Object.keys(s.stretchRounds || {}).length > 0);
  const completionRate = recent.length === 0 ? 0 : recent.reduce((acc, s) => {
    const keys = Object.keys(s.stretchRounds || {});
    const done = keys.filter((k) => normalizeStretchRounds(s.stretchRounds[k], activeStretches.find((c) => c.key === k)) > 0).length;
    return acc + done / keys.length;
  }, 0) / recent.length;

  if (completionRate < 0.7) {
    return { ready: false, reason: "Finish the current stretch routine consistently for a few sessions before adding more to it." };
  }

  const next = candidates[0];
  return { ready: true, suggestion: next, reason: next.why };
}

// ---------- coach engine ----------
// Double-progression style recommendation: push reps to the top of the
// range before adding weight, back off cleanly after a struggle, and
// flag exercises that have stalled so they don't just sit unchanged forever.
function getExerciseRecommendation(sessions, key, exercises) {
  const cfg = exercises[key];
  if (!cfg) return null;
  const history = sessions
    .filter((s) => s.exercises && s.exercises[key])
    .map((s) => ({ date: s.date, ...s.exercises[key] }));

  // Once a real kettlebell weight has actually been logged, the exercise has
  // "graduated" out of the bodyweight phase even if its base config still
  // says noWeight — fall through to normal weighted double progression below.
  const graduated = cfg.noWeight && history.length > 0 && history[history.length - 1].weight;

  if (cfg.noWeight && !graduated) {
    // Bodyweight / reps-per-side movements (e.g. Turkish getup): progress reps first.
    if (history.length === 0) {
      return { weight: null, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: cfg.targetReps, action: "start", rationale: `Starting point: ${cfg.targetReps} reps per side, focus on form.` };
    }
    const last = history[history.length - 1];
    const secondLast = history[history.length - 2];
    const repCap = cfg.targetReps + 3;
    if (last.feel === "struggled" || last.feel === "shaky") {
      return { weight: null, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: Math.max(cfg.targetReps, (last.reps ?? cfg.targetReps) - 1), action: "hold", rationale: "Last rep felt unstable — holding here to groove the pattern before adding reps." };
    }
    if ((last.feel === "easy" || last.feel === "solid") && secondLast && (secondLast.feel === "easy" || secondLast.feel === "solid")) {
      const atCap = (last.reps ?? cfg.targetReps) >= repCap;
      if (atCap) {
        const startWeight = cfg.kettlebellStart ?? 15;
        return { weight: startWeight, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: cfg.targetReps, action: "weight-ready", rationale: `Reps solid at the top of this progression for two sessions in a row — time to add a kettlebell. Starting at ${startWeight}${cfg.unit} and dropping back to ${cfg.targetReps} reps per side, since the load is what's new now.` };
      }
      const reps = Math.min(repCap, (last.reps ?? cfg.targetReps) + 1);
      return { weight: null, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps, action: "reps-up", rationale: `Two clean sessions — adding a rep per side (${reps}).` };
    }
    return { weight: null, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: last.reps ?? cfg.targetReps, action: "hold", rationale: "Steady — repeat this rep count." };
  }

  const effectiveStep = cfg.step || 5;
  const repLow = cfg.targetReps;
  const repHigh = cfg.noWeight ? repLow : cfg.targetReps + 4;

  if (history.length === 0) {
    return { weight: 0, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: repLow, action: "start", rationale: "No history yet — start light, find a working weight in set 1, and log how it feels." };
  }

  const last = history[history.length - 1];
  const secondLast = history[history.length - 2];
  const lastReps = last.reps ?? repLow;

  // Some movements do nothing at all below a certain load — an unloaded hip
  // thrust is the clearest case, where three sets of twelve is a warm-up rather
  // than a working set. loadFloor is that threshold. Laddering reps up from zero
  // would take months to arrive somewhere the exercise should have started, so
  // the engine steps straight to the floor once the movement is being tolerated.
  if (cfg.loadFloor && (last.weight ?? 0) < cfg.loadFloor && last.feel !== "struggled") {
    return {
      weight: cfg.loadFloor, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: repLow, action: "load-floor",
      rationale: `Bodyweight on the floor sits under the load this movement needs to train anything — moving to the bench version at ${cfg.loadFloor}${cfg.unit} and resetting to ${repLow} reps. Expect it to feel like real work now.`,
    };
  }

  if (last.feel === "struggled") {
    const priorStable = [...history].reverse().find((h) => h.feel === "solid" || h.feel === "easy");
    const weight = priorStable ? priorStable.weight : Math.max(0, (last.weight ?? effectiveStep) - effectiveStep);
    return { weight, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: repLow, action: "deload", rationale: `Backed off to ${weight}${cfg.unit} and reset reps to ${repLow} after a tough session — rebuild from a stable base.` };
  }

  if (last.feel === "easy") {
    if (secondLast && secondLast.feel === "easy") {
      if (lastReps < repHigh) {
        const reps = Math.min(repHigh, lastReps + 2);
        return { weight: last.weight, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps, action: "reps-up", rationale: `Two easy sessions in a row — holding ${last.weight}${cfg.unit} and pushing reps to ${reps} before adding weight.` };
      }
      const weight = last.weight + effectiveStep;
      return { weight, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: repLow, action: "weight-up", rationale: `Maxed reps at ${repHigh} — stepping up to ${weight}${cfg.unit} and resetting reps to ${repLow}.` };
    }
    return { weight: last.weight, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: lastReps, action: "hold", rationale: "Felt easy — one more session like this and it's time to progress." };
  }

  if (last.feel === "solid") {
    return { weight: last.weight, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: lastReps, action: "hold", rationale: "Right in the working zone — repeat this to build consistency before pushing further." };
  }

  // "tough but completed" or anything else non-struggled
  return { weight: last.weight, sets: cfg.targetSets, reps: Math.max(repLow, lastReps - 2), action: "hold", rationale: "Tough but completed — hold steady here rather than adding difficulty." };
}

// Thin wrapper kept for call sites that only need the weight number.
function suggestNextWeight(sessions, key, exercises) {
  const rec = getExerciseRecommendation(sessions, key, exercises);
  return rec ? rec.weight : null;
}

// Flags an exercise as plateaued when weight AND reps have both sat still
// across the last several logged sessions despite mixed/struggled results —
// a sign that more of the same isn't working and something should change.
function checkPlateau(sessions, key, exercises) {
  const cfg = exercises[key];
  if (!cfg) return null;
  const history = sessions
    .filter((s) => s.exercises && s.exercises[key])
    .map((s) => s.exercises[key]);
  const graduated = cfg.noWeight && history.length > 0 && history[history.length - 1].weight;
  if (cfg.noWeight && !graduated) return null;
  if (history.length < 4) return null;
  const recent = history.slice(-4);
  const sameWeight = recent.every((h) => h.weight === recent[0].weight);
  const sameReps = recent.every((h) => (h.reps ?? cfg.targetReps) === (recent[0].reps ?? cfg.targetReps));
  const anyStruggle = recent.some((h) => h.feel === "struggled");
  if (sameWeight && sameReps && anyStruggle) {
    return `${cfg.label} has sat at ${recent[0].weight}${cfg.unit} × ${recent[0].reps ?? cfg.targetReps} for 4 sessions with at least one struggle — consider a deload session at 80% for one week, or swapping in a different movement for 2 weeks before returning to it.`;
  }
  return null;
}

// Suggests a next cardio target from the trend across recent runs rather than
// from the last one. Anchoring on the last run means a single short day rewrites
// the baseline and the next target reads as a demotion, when nothing about the
// underlying fitness has changed. So: take the last several comparable runs,
// anchor the distance on the best of them, and let the direction of pace and
// heart rate across the window decide whether to push, hold or ease off. Runs
// marked time-constrained are left out of this entirely — they are still
// logged, they just don't get a vote on what the baseline is.
const CARDIO_WINDOW = 6;

function cardioPace(s) {
  return s.duration / s.distance;
}

function cardioMean(xs) {
  return xs.length ? xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length : null;
}

// Treadmill and outdoor are one running target, not two. They're the same
// stimulus in the same units, and keeping separate windows meant a first
// outdoor run read as a brand new modality and got told to build a 1.5mi
// baseline while treadmill runs were already at 1.9mi. Pool is yards and
// stays its own pool.
function cardioGroup(modality) {
  return (modality || "treadmill") === "pool" ? "pool" : "run";
}

function suggestCardioTarget(sessions, modality = "treadmill") {
  // Only compare like with like — pool yardage and running miles aren't the
  // same units, and pace between them means nothing.
  const group = cardioGroup(modality);
  const groupHistory = sessions
    .filter((s) => s.distance && s.duration && cardioGroup(s.modality) === group && !s.timeConstrained);
  const surfaceHistory = groupHistory.filter((s) => (s.modality || "treadmill") === modality);
  // Compare like with like where there is enough of it, but never start a new
  // surface from a cold baseline. Treadmill runs sit inside a gym session and
  // outdoor runs don't, so their distances aren't interchangeable even though
  // they're the same sport — a 3.1mi outdoor run must never set the treadmill's
  // distance, or it lands a 49-minute run in a 30-minute slot. Below two runs on
  // a surface there's nothing to read, and the rest of your running is a far
  // better starting point than telling someone already at 1.9mi to build a
  // 1.5mi baseline.
  const history = (surfaceHistory.length >= 2 ? surfaceHistory : groupHistory).slice(-CARDIO_WINDOW);
  if (modality === "pool" && history.length < 2) {
    return { distance: 500, duration: 25, rationale: "New modality — swim easy and find a baseline. Distance in yards." };
  }
  if (history.length < 2) {
    return { distance: 1.5, duration: 30, rationale: "Building a baseline — aim for a comfortable, sustainable pace." };
  }
  const yards = modality === "pool";
  const unit = yards ? "yd" : "mi";
  const round = (d) => (yards ? Math.round(d / 25) * 25 : Math.round(d * 100) / 100);
  const bump = yards ? 50 : 0.1;

  // Anchor on the middle of the recent block, not the peak. Anchoring on the
  // best run ratchets: one unusually good day raises the floor permanently,
  // ordinary variation gets read as a new sustainable baseline, and the target
  // can only ever climb. Every training-load framework that has survived
  // scrutiny references a rolling average or median rather than a maximum, and
  // the median in particular shrugs off a single outlier in either direction.
  const sorted = history.map((s) => s.distance).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const anchor = sorted.length % 2
    ? sorted[(sorted.length - 1) / 2]
    : (sorted[sorted.length / 2 - 1] + sorted[sorted.length / 2]) / 2;

  // Split the window in half and compare the halves. A single run is noise;
  // the direction across several is the thing worth responding to.
  const half = Math.ceil(history.length / 2);
  const older = history.slice(0, history.length - half);
  const newer = history.slice(history.length - half);
  const olderPace = cardioMean(older.map(cardioPace));
  const newerPace = cardioMean(newer.map(cardioPace));
  const hrOf = (arr) => cardioMean(arr.map((s) => s.hrAvg).filter(Boolean));
  const olderHr = hrOf(older);
  const newerHr = hrOf(newer);

  // Plan the clock at the recent sustainable pace rather than the pace of the
  // best run — a one-off fast day shouldn't set the duration.
  const pace = newerPace;
  const n = history.length;

  // One noisy signal isn't a reason to hold back. Average heart rate between
  // sessions moves with heat, sleep, caffeine, and how hard the lifting was
  // beforehand, and a 5% shift across three runs sits inside ordinary
  // variation. Requiring pace and heart rate to tell the same story — slower
  // and higher together — is far harder to trip by accident.
  const hrUp = olderHr && newerHr ? newerHr > olderHr * 1.05 : false;
  const paceSlower = olderPace ? newerPace > olderPace * 1.05 : false;
  const paceFaster = olderPace ? newerPace < olderPace * 0.98 : false;
  const labouring = hrUp && paceSlower;

  // Advance on consistency, not on a good day, and never because pace improved.
  // Moving distance up on the back of a pace gain advances volume and intensity
  // at the same time, which is the one combination endurance practice is
  // unanimous about separating. A faster pace over the same ground is fitness
  // banked — it isn't permission to go further as well.
  const recent = history.slice(-3);
  const holdingTheLine = recent.length >= 3 && recent.every((s) => s.distance >= anchor);
  const readyToStep = history.length >= 4 && holdingTheLine && !labouring;

  const anchorLabel = round(anchor);
  if (labouring) {
    const distance = round(anchor);
    return { distance, duration: Math.round(distance * pace), rationale: `Pace has slipped and heart rate has climbed together across your last ${n} runs — that pairing is worth listening to. Holding at ${anchorLabel}${unit} until it settles.` };
  }
  if (readyToStep) {
    const distance = round(anchor * 1.05);
    return { distance, duration: Math.round(distance * pace), rationale: `Your last three runs all met or beat your recent ${anchorLabel}${unit} — a small step up to ${distance}${unit}.` };
  }
  const distance = round(anchor);
  const note = paceFaster
    ? ` Pace is trending faster, which is worth banking rather than spending — same distance, less effort.`
    : ``;
  return { distance, duration: Math.round(distance * pace), rationale: `Settling in around your recent ${anchorLabel}${unit} across your last ${n} runs.${note}` };
}

// Builds a plain-language weekly coaching summary tied to the user's
// stated goals, pulling together cardio targets, per-exercise directives,
// and any plateau flags into one place.
function computeCoachSummary(sessions, exercises, plan, sessionType, allStretches = STRETCHES) {
  // Mirror the wizard's filter exactly, so the plan shown on Today is the
  // same work the log flow will actually walk through.
  const activeKeys = Object.keys(exercises).filter((k) => {
    const cfg = exercises[k];
    if (cfg.active === false) return false;
    if (!sessionType) return true;
    if (!cfg.sessionType || cfg.sessionType === "both") return true;
    return cfg.sessionType === sessionType;
  });
  const directives = activeKeys.map((k) => ({
    key: k,
    label: exercises[k].label,
    rec: getExerciseRecommendation(sessions, k, exercises),
  }));
  const plateaus = activeKeys.map((k) => checkPlateau(sessions, k, exercises)).filter(Boolean);
  const cardio = suggestCardioTarget(sessions);
  const readiness = assessChamberReadiness(sessions);
  const rest = assessRestPattern(sessions);
  const balance = assessSessionBalance(sessions, exercises);
  const stretchFreq = assessStretchFrequency(sessions);
  const stretches = (sessionType ? selectSessionStretches(sessionType, allStretches, sessions) : orderStretches(allStretches)).map((cfg) => ({
    key: cfg.key,
    label: cfg.label,
    cfg,
    rec: getStretchRecommendation(sessions, cfg),
  }));
  return { directives, plateaus, cardio, readiness, rest, balance, stretches, stretchFreq, goals: plan.goals };
}

function StepDots({ step, total }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, justifyContent: "center", margin: "18px 0" }}>
      {Array.from({ length: total }).map((_, i) => (
        <div key={i} style={{
          width: i === step ? 20 : 6, height: 6, borderRadius: 0,
          background: i === step ? "var(--activity)" : "#1B1B1B",
          transition: "all .25s ease"
        }} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function Chip({ active, onClick, children, color }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      style={{
        padding: "10px 16px",
        borderRadius: 0,
        border: `1.5px solid ${active ? (color || "var(--activity)") : "#1B1B1B"}`,
        background: active ? (color || "var(--activity)") : "#F9F6EE",
        color: active ? "#F9F6EE" : "#1B1B1B",
        fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif",
        fontSize: 14,
        fontWeight: 500,
        cursor: "pointer",
        transition: "all .15s ease",
        textAlign: "left",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

// A full-size Stepper is 40 + 14 + 110 + 14 + 40 = 218px. Two side by side need
// 446px, but a Card on a 360-414px phone only offers ~270-330px of content width,
// so paired steppers used to shoot off both edges of the screen. Compact mode keeps
// the 40px tap targets, tightens the gaps, and stacks the unit under the numeral so
// the readout needs ~40px instead of ~110. Never give the readout a fixed minWidth
// in compact mode — it has to be the part that yields when the card gets narrow.
function Stepper({ value, onChange, step = 5, unit = "lb", min = 0, compact = false }) {
  const safe = typeof value === "number" && !Number.isNaN(value) ? value : min;
  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center",
      gap: compact ? 2 : 14, minWidth: 0, maxWidth: "100%",
    }}>
      <button onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(min, safe - step))} style={btnCircle} aria-label={`Decrease ${unit}`}>
        <Minus size={18} />
      </button>
      <div style={{
        fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B",
        fontSize: compact ? 21 : 30, lineHeight: compact ? 1.05 : 1.2,
        minWidth: compact ? 0 : 110, flex: compact ? "1 1 0" : "0 0 auto",
        textAlign: "center", overflow: "hidden", whiteSpace: "nowrap",
      }}>
        {safe}
        {compact ? (
          <div style={{
            fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: 500, color: "#6E6759",
            letterSpacing: 0.7, textTransform: "uppercase", marginTop: 1,
          }}>{unit}</div>
        ) : (
          <span style={{ fontSize: 15, color: "#6E6759", marginLeft: 4 }}>{unit}</span>
        )}
      </div>
      <button onClick={() => onChange(safe + step)} style={btnCircle} aria-label={`Increase ${unit}`}>
        <Plus size={18} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

// Lays two (or one) steppers out as equal grid tracks. minmax(0, 1fr) is what stops
// the overflow: grid items default to min-width:auto and will happily push past the
// container otherwise. Children are given `compact` automatically when there's more
// than one, so callers never have to think about it.
function StepperPair({ children }) {
  const items = React.Children.toArray(children);
  if (items.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "grid", width: "100%", alignItems: "center",
      gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${items.length}, minmax(0, 1fr))`,
      // Must stay clearly wider than the 2px gap inside each Stepper, or the four
      // buttons read as one evenly-spaced row and you can't tell which +/- is which.
      gap: items.length > 1 ? 16 : 0,
    }}>
      {items.map((child, i) => (
        <div key={i} style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
          {React.isValidElement(child) ? React.cloneElement(child, { compact: items.length > 1 }) : child}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

const btnCircle = {
  width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: "50%", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B",
  background: "#F9F6EE", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center",
  cursor: "pointer", color: "#1B1B1B", flex: "0 0 auto", padding: 0,
};

const btnPrimary = {
  padding: "12px 22px", borderRadius: 0, border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B",
  background: "#1B1B1B", color: "#F9F6EE", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif",
  fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 14, cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
  boxShadow: "4px 4px 0 #75695A",
};

const btnGhost = {
  padding: "12px 18px", borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B",
  background: "transparent", color: "#1B1B1B", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif",
  fontWeight: 500, fontSize: 14, cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
};

// A verdict is a judgement, not a tag, so it reads as a rubber stamp pressed
// onto the card. The tilt is the only thing in the app allowed off-axis.
const stampStyle = {
  display: "inline-block", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", background: "transparent",
  fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 11,
  letterSpacing: 1.3, textTransform: "uppercase", padding: "4px 9px",
  transform: "rotate(-5deg)", marginRight: 4,
};

// Notes the cook wrote, set apart from app-authored text by a blue rule and
// typewriter italics rather than a script face.
const annotationStyle = {
  fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontStyle: "italic", fontSize: 12,
  color: "var(--activity)", marginTop: 10, lineHeight: 1.55,
  borderLeft: "3px solid var(--activity)", paddingLeft: 9,
};

// ---------- log wizard ----------
function LogWizard({ sessions, exercises, stretches = STRETCHES, initialDraft, onCancel, onComplete }) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(initialDraft && Number.isFinite(initialDraft.step) ? initialDraft.step : 0);
  const [modality, setModality] = useState((initialDraft && initialDraft.entry && initialDraft.entry.modality) || "treadmill");
  const modalityCfg = MODALITIES.find((m) => m.key === modality) || MODALITIES[0];
  const cardioTarget = useMemo(() => suggestCardioTarget(sessions, modality), [sessions, modality]);
  const defaultType = useMemo(() => {
    const lastReal = [...sessions].reverse().find(s => effectiveType(s, exercises) === "A" || effectiveType(s, exercises) === "B");
    return effectiveType(lastReal || {}, exercises) === "A" ? "B" : "A";
  }, [sessions, exercises]);
  const [entry, setEntry] = useState((initialDraft && initialDraft.entry) || {
    date: localDateStr(),
    type: defaultType,
    duration: cardioTarget.duration, distance: cardioTarget.distance, hrAvg: 130, hrMax: 150,
    modality: "treadmill",
    timeConstrained: false,
    warmup: null,
    exercises: {}, bodyweight: {}, stretchRounds: {}, sauna: null, meditation: null, notes: "",
  });

  // Write the whole in-progress session out on every change. Cheap (one small
  // JSON blob) and it means nothing typed is ever held only in memory.
  useEffect(() => {
    saveDraft({ entry, step, savedAt: Date.now() });
  }, [entry, step]);

  // An exercise belongs in this session if it's active and either matches the
  // session type or is marked for both. Exercises with no sessionType set
  // (older saved configs, custom additions) default to appearing in both.
  // A run-only day has no lifting to plan and no movement prep to do, so the
  // whole middle of the wizard drops out rather than being walked past.
  const isRun = entry.type === "R";

  const plannedExercises = isRun ? [] : Object.keys(exercises).filter((k) => {
    const cfg = exercises[k];
    if (cfg.active === false) return false;
    if (!cfg.sessionType || cfg.sessionType === "both") return true;
    return cfg.sessionType === entry.type;
  });

  const sessionStretches = useMemo(() => selectSessionStretches(entry.type, stretches, sessions), [entry.type, stretches, sessions]);
  const stretchPool = useMemo(() => stretchesForSession(entry.type, stretches), [entry.type, stretches]);

  const steps = isRun
    ? ["cardio", "hr", "bodyweight", "stretches", "closer", "review"]
    : ["cardio", "hr", "warmup", ...plannedExercises, "stretches", "closer", "review"];
  const total = steps.length;
  const current = steps[step];

  const next = () => setStep((s) => Math.min(total - 1, s + 1));
  const back = () => setStep((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1));

  const setExercise = (key, field, value) => {
    setEntry((e) => {
      const cfg = exercises[key];
      const rec = getExerciseRecommendation(sessions, key, exercises);
      const existing = e.exercises[key] || {
        weight: rec?.weight ?? 0,
        sets: rec?.sets ?? cfg.targetSets,
        reps: rec?.reps ?? cfg.targetReps,
        feel: null,
      };
      return {
        ...e,
        exercises: { ...e.exercises, [key]: { ...existing, [field]: value } },
      };
    });
  };

  const skipExercise = (key) => {
    setEntry((e) => {
      const copy = { ...e.exercises };
      delete copy[key];
      return { ...e, exercises: copy };
    });
    next();
  };

  const finish = () => {
    onComplete({ id: uid(), ...entry });
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 480, margin: "0 auto" }}>
      <StepDots step={step} total={total} />

      {current === "cardio" && (
        <Card title="Treadmill / Run" sub={`Target: ${cardioTarget.distance}mi / ${cardioTarget.duration} min`}>
          <Field label="Date">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, justifyContent: "center", flexWrap: "wrap", marginBottom: 10 }}>
              {[0, 1, 2].map((back) => {
                const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() - back);
                const ds = localDateStr(d);
                return (
                  <Chip key={back} active={entry.date === ds} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, date: ds }))}>
                    {back === 0 ? "Today" : back === 1 ? "Yesterday" : "2 days ago"}
                  </Chip>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <input
              type="date"
              value={entry.date}
              max={localDateStr()}
              onChange={(e) => e.target.value && setEntry((prev) => ({ ...prev, date: e.target.value }))}
              style={{
                width: "100%", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 12px",
                fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 14, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
              }}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Session">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, justifyContent: "center" }}>
              <Chip active={entry.type === "A"} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, type: "A" }))}>Session A</Chip>
              <Chip active={entry.type === "B"} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, type: "B" }))}>Session B</Chip>
              <Chip active={entry.type === "R"} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, type: "R" }))}>Run only</Chip>
            </div>
          </Field>
          <Field label="Cardio type">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, justifyContent: "center", flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
              {MODALITIES.map((m) => (
                <Chip key={m.key} active={modality === m.key} onClick={() => {
                  setModality(m.key);
                  const target = suggestCardioTarget(sessions, m.key);
                  setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, modality: m.key, distance: target.distance, duration: target.duration }));
                }}>{m.label}</Chip>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Field>
          <div style={{
            fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "var(--activity)", lineHeight: 1.6,
            background: "var(--activity-tint)", border: "2px solid var(--activity)", borderRadius: 0, padding: "10px 14px", marginBottom: 4,
          }}>
            Coach: {cardioTarget.rationale}
          </div>
          <Field label="Duration (min : sec)">
            <StepperPair>
              <Stepper value={Math.floor(entry.duration)} step={1} unit="min" min={0}
                onChange={(v) => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, duration: v + (e.duration % 1) }))} />
              <Stepper value={Math.round((entry.duration % 1) * 60)} step={15} unit="sec" min={0}
                onChange={(v) => setEntry((e) => {
                  const mins = Math.floor(e.duration);
                  if (v >= 60) return { ...e, duration: mins + 1 };
                  if (v < 0) return { ...e, duration: Math.max(0, mins - 1) + 45 / 60 };
                  return { ...e, duration: mins + v / 60 };
                })} />
            </StepperPair>
          </Field>
          <Field label={`Distance (${modalityCfg.unit})`}>
            <Stepper value={entry.distance} step={modalityCfg.step} unit={modalityCfg.unit} min={0}
              onChange={(v) => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, distance: modalityCfg.unit === "yd" ? Math.round(v) : Math.round(v * 100) / 100 }))} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Was this a full run?">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, justifyContent: "center" }}>
              <Chip active={!entry.timeConstrained} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, timeConstrained: false }))}>Full run</Chip>
              <Chip active={!!entry.timeConstrained} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, timeConstrained: true }))}>Short on time</Chip>
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 8, lineHeight: 1.5, textAlign: "center" }}>
              A short-on-time run is logged in full but left out of the target maths, so cutting one session short doesn't reset what you're working from.
            </div>
          </Field>
        </Card>
      )}

      {current === "hr" && (
        <Card title="Heart Rate" sub="Always tracked">
          <Field label="Average HR">
            <Stepper value={entry.hrAvg} step={1} unit="bpm" onChange={(v) => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, hrAvg: v }))} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Max HR">
            <Stepper value={entry.hrMax} step={1} unit="bpm" onChange={(v) => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, hrMax: v }))} />
          </Field>
        </Card>
      )}

      {current === "warmup" && (
        <Card title="Movement Prep" sub="Before lifting · about 3 minutes">
          <div style={{
            fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "var(--activity)", lineHeight: 1.6,
            background: "var(--activity-tint)", border: "2px solid var(--activity)", borderRadius: 0, padding: "10px 14px", marginBottom: 4,
          }}>
            Coach: dynamic movement before lifting primes the muscles you're about to load. Save the long static holds for after — held cold, they temporarily reduce strength output.
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
            {WARMUP.map((w) => (
              <div key={w.key} style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 8 }}>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 13.5, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{w.label}</div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 1 }}>{w.detail}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <Field label="Did you do movement prep?">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, justifyContent: "center" }}>
              {COMPLETION_OPTIONS.map((o) => (
                <Chip key={o.key} active={entry.warmup === o.key} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, warmup: o.key }))}>{o.label}</Chip>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Field>
        </Card>
      )}

      {current === "bodyweight" && (
        <Card title="Bodyweight Work" sub="Optional · anything you did without a gym">
          <div style={{
            fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "var(--activity)", lineHeight: 1.6,
            background: "var(--activity-tint)", border: "2px solid var(--activity)", borderRadius: 0, padding: "10px 14px", marginBottom: 4,
          }}>
            Coach: this is logged as work done, not as a strength session — it won't count toward the A/B rotation, and leaving it all at zero costs nothing.
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
            {BODYWEIGHT.map((b) => (
              <div key={b.key}>
                <Field label={`${b.label} (${b.unit})`}>
                  <Stepper
                    value={(entry.bodyweight || {})[b.key] || 0}
                    step={b.step}
                    unit={b.unit === "sec" ? "sec" : "reps"}
                    min={0}
                    onChange={(v) => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, bodyweight: { ...(e.bodyweight || {}), [b.key]: v } }))}
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {plannedExercises.includes(current) && (
        <ExerciseStep
          exKey={current}
          sessions={sessions}
          exercises={exercises}
          entry={entry}
          setExercise={setExercise}
          onSkip={() => skipExercise(current)}
        />
      )}

      {current === "stretches" && (
        <Card title="Stretch Routine" sub={isRun ? "After the run, while warm · optional" : `After training, while warm · Session ${entry.type} emphasis`}>
          <div style={{
            fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "var(--activity)", lineHeight: 1.6,
            background: "var(--activity-tint)", border: "2px solid var(--activity)", borderRadius: 0, padding: "10px 14px", marginBottom: 4,
          }}>
            Coach: rounds build toward roughly {STRETCH_TARGET_ACCUM}s total per muscle group — the point where added time stops paying off. Log the rounds you actually did.
            {stretchPool.length > sessionStretches.length && (
              <> {" "}{sessionStretches.length} of your {stretchPool.length} tonight: hip flexors, hamstrings and calves every session, the rest rotating in by whichever is most overdue.</>
            )}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 14 }}>
            {sessionStretches.map((s) => {
              const rec = getStretchRecommendation(sessions, s);
              const val = normalizeStretchRounds(entry.stretchRounds[s.key], s);
              return (
                <div key={s.key} style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 10 }}>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "baseline", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 14, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{s.label}</div>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12, color: "var(--activity)" }}>
                      {s.holdSeconds}s{s.perSide ? "/side" : ""} × {rec.rounds}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 2, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                    {s.muscle} · {rec.rationale}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, marginTop: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                    {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((r) => (
                      <button key={r} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, stretchRounds: { ...e.stretchRounds, [s.key]: r } }))}
                        style={{
                          padding: "6px 12px", borderRadius: 0, fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer",
                          fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif",
                          border: `1.5px solid ${val === r ? "var(--activity)" : "#1B1B1B"}`,
                          background: val === r ? "var(--activity)" : "#F9F6EE",
                          color: val === r ? "#F9F6EE" : "#1B1B1B",
                        }}>{r === 0 ? "skipped" : `${r} round${r === 1 ? "" : "s"}`}</button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {current === "closer" && (
        <Card title="Sauna & Meditation" sub="Your usual closer">
          <Field label="Sauna">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              {COMPLETION_OPTIONS.map((c) => (
                <Chip key={c.key} active={entry.sauna === c.key} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, sauna: c.key }))}>{c.label}</Chip>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Field>
          <Field label="Meditation">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              {COMPLETION_OPTIONS.map((c) => (
                <Chip key={c.key} active={entry.meditation === c.key} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, meditation: c.key }))}>{c.label}</Chip>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Field>
          <Field label="Anything else worth noting?">
            <textarea
              value={entry.notes}
              onChange={(ev) => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, notes: ev.target.value }))}
              placeholder="Optional — form notes, how it felt, etc."
              style={{
                width: "100%", minHeight: 70, borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B",
                padding: 10, fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 14, resize: "vertical",
                background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
              }}
            />
          </Field>
        </Card>
      )}

      {current === "review" && (
        <Card title="Review & save" sub="Here's what's going in the log">
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.9 }}>
            <div>{isRun ? "Run only" : `Session ${entry.type}`} · {entry.date}</div>
            <div>
              {entry.duration} min · {entry.distance} {modalityCfg.unit} · HR {entry.hrAvg}/{entry.hrMax}
              {entry.timeConstrained ? " · short on time" : ""}
            </div>
            {Object.entries(entry.exercises).map(([k, v]) => (
              <div key={k}>{exercises[k]?.label || k}: {v.weight ?? 0}{exercises[k]?.unit} × {v.sets ?? 1}×{v.reps} — {v.feel}</div>
            ))}
            {BODYWEIGHT.filter((b) => ((entry.bodyweight || {})[b.key] || 0) > 0).map((b) => (
              <div key={b.key}>{b.label}: {entry.bodyweight[b.key]} {b.unit}</div>
            ))}
            {!isRun && <div>Movement prep: {entry.warmup || "not set"}</div>}
            <div>
              Stretches: {sessionStretches.filter((s) => normalizeStretchRounds(entry.stretchRounds[s.key], s) > 0).length}/{sessionStretches.length} done
              {" · "}
              {sessionStretches.reduce((sum, s) => sum + stretchAccum(s, normalizeStretchRounds(entry.stretchRounds[s.key], s) || 0), 0)}s total
            </div>
            <div>Sauna: {entry.sauna || "not set"} · Meditation: {entry.meditation || "not set"}</div>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginTop: 22 }}>
        <button style={btnGhost} onClick={step === 0 ? onCancel : back}>
          <ChevronLeft size={16} /> {step === 0 ? "Cancel" : "Back"}
        </button>
        {current === "review" ? (
          <button style={btnPrimary} onClick={finish}><Check size={16} /> Save session</button>
        ) : (
          <button style={btnPrimary} onClick={next}>Next <ChevronRight size={16} /></button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ExerciseStep({ exKey, sessions, exercises, entry, setExercise, onSkip }) {
  const cfg = exercises[exKey];
  const rec = getExerciseRecommendation(sessions, exKey, exercises);
  const val = entry.exercises[exKey] || {
    weight: rec?.weight ?? 0, sets: rec?.sets ?? cfg.targetSets, reps: rec?.reps ?? cfg.targetReps, feel: null,
  };
  const [showHowTo, setShowHowTo] = useState(false);

  const showWeight = rec?.weight != null;
  return (
    <Card title={cfg.label} sub={showWeight ? `Planned: ${rec?.weight ?? "—"}${cfg.unit} × ${rec?.sets ?? cfg.targetSets}×${rec?.reps ?? cfg.targetReps}` : `Planned: ${rec?.reps ?? cfg.targetReps} reps / side`}>
      {rec?.rationale && (
        <div style={{
          fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "var(--activity)", lineHeight: 1.6,
          background: "var(--activity-tint)", border: "2px solid var(--activity)", borderRadius: 0, padding: "10px 14px", marginBottom: 4,
        }}>
          Coach: {rec.rationale}
        </div>
      )}
      {cfg.explainer && (
        <div>
          <button onClick={() => setShowHowTo((v) => !v)} style={{ ...btnGhost, width: "100%", justifyContent: "space-between", padding: "10px 14px" }}>
            <span>How to do this</span><span>{showHowTo ? "−" : "+"}</span>
          </button>
          {showHowTo && (
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.7, background: "#E7E0D0", borderRadius: 0, padding: 14, marginTop: 8, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }}>
              {cfg.explainer}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {showWeight && (
        <Field label="Weight used">
          <Stepper value={val.weight} step={cfg.step || 5} unit={cfg.unit} onChange={(v) => setExercise(exKey, "weight", v)} />
        </Field>
      )}
      <Field label={showWeight ? "Sets × Reps" : "Reps per side"}>
        <StepperPair>
          {showWeight && (
            <Stepper value={val.sets} step={1} unit="sets" min={1} onChange={(v) => setExercise(exKey, "sets", v)} />
          )}
          <Stepper value={val.reps} step={1} unit="reps" min={1} onChange={(v) => setExercise(exKey, "reps", v)} />
        </StepperPair>
      </Field>
      <Field label="How'd it feel?">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
          {FEEL_OPTIONS.map((f) => (
            <Chip key={f.key} active={val.feel === f.key} color={f.color} onClick={() => setExercise(exKey, "feel", f.key)}>
              {f.label}
            </Chip>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Field>
      <ExerciseQA label={cfg.label} />
      <button onClick={onSkip} style={{ ...btnGhost, marginTop: 4, width: "100%", justifyContent: "center" }}>
        Skipped this exercise today
      </button>
    </Card>
  );
}

function ExerciseQA({ label }) {
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState("");
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const ask = async () => {
    if (!question.trim()) return;
    setLoading(true);
    setAnswer("");
    try {
      const text = await askClaude(`Question about the "${label}" gym exercise: ${question}. Answer briefly and practically, 3-4 sentences max.`);
      setAnswer(text);
    } catch (e) {
      setAnswer(e.message === "NO_API_KEY" ? "No API key set — add one via the key icon at the top of the app." : "Couldn't reach the assistant — check your API key or try again.");
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 14, marginTop: 4 }}>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: "#6E6759", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.5, marginBottom: 8 }}>
        Ask about this exercise
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
        <input
          value={question}
          onChange={(e) => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && ask()}
          placeholder="e.g. why does my grip fail first?"
          style={{
            flex: 1, borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 12px",
            fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
          }}
        />
        <button onClick={ask} disabled={loading} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "10px 16px" }}>
          {loading ? "…" : "Ask"}
        </button>
      </div>
      {answer && (
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6, marginTop: 10 }}>
          {answer}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Card({ title, sub, children }) {
  return (
    <div style={{
      background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0,
      padding: 24,
    }}>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{title}</div>
      {sub && <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 2, marginBottom: 16 }}>{sub}</div>}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 18 }}>{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({ label, children }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: "#6E6759", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.5, marginBottom: 8, textAlign: "center" }}>{label}</div>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

// ---------- progression ladder (signature element) ----------
function ProgressionLadder({ sessions, exKey, exercises }) {
  const cfg = exercises[exKey];
  const data = sessions
    .filter((s) => s.exercises && s.exercises[exKey])
    .map((s, i) => ({ session: i + 1, weight: s.exercises[exKey].weight ?? 0, feel: s.exercises[exKey].feel }));

  if (data.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: "18px 20px" }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline", marginBottom: 8 }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{cfg.label}</div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 13, color: "var(--activity)", fontWeight: 600 }}>
          {data[data.length - 1].weight}{cfg.unit}
        </div>
      </div>
      <Sparkline data={data.map((d) => d.weight)} width={340} height={64} color="var(--activity)" />
    </div>
  );
}

function Sparkline({ data, width = 300, height = 60, color = "var(--activity)" }) {
  if (!data || data.length === 0) return null;
  const min = Math.min(...data);
  const max = Math.max(...data);
  const pad = (max - min) * 0.15 || 5;
  const lo = min - pad;
  const hi = max + pad;
  const n = data.length;
  const stepX = n > 1 ? width / (n - 1) : 0;
  const toY = (v) => height - ((v - lo) / (hi - lo || 1)) * height;

  // step-after path: horizontal then vertical between points
  let path = `M 0 ${toY(data[0]).toFixed(1)}`;
  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {
    const x = (i * stepX).toFixed(1);
    const yPrev = toY(data[i - 1]).toFixed(1);
    const yCur = toY(data[i]).toFixed(1);
    path += ` L ${x} ${yPrev} L ${x} ${yCur}`;
  }

  return (
    <svg width="100%" height={height} viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} preserveAspectRatio="none" style={{ display: "block" }}>
      <path d={path} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinejoin="round" />
      {data.map((v, i) => (
        <circle key={i} cx={(i * stepX).toFixed(1)} cy={toY(v).toFixed(1)} r="3" fill={color} />
      ))}
    </svg>
  );
}

// ---------- running ----------
// The lift ladders plot weight, which genuinely holds flat and then jumps, so a
// step line tells the truth about them. Run distance moves every single session,
// so drawing runs the same way would be a jagged line wearing a staircase
// costume. What actually steps in the cardio system is the target, so the target
// gets the step line and the runs get plotted as what they are: points scattered
// around it. Pace rides underneath on its own scale, inverted so that a faster
// run reads as higher up, which is the only way it can sit next to a rising
// target line without looking like the two disagree.
// Every run ever logged is plotted. No cap: the whole history is the point,
// and the dots shrink to make room rather than the oldest ones being dropped.
function runSeries(sessions) {
  const out = [];
  sessions.forEach((s, i) => {
    const modality = s.modality || "treadmill";
    if (cardioGroup(modality) !== "run") return;
    if (!s.distance || !s.duration) return;
    out.push({
      index: i,
      date: s.date,
      modality,
      distance: s.distance,
      pace: cardioPace(s),
      short: !!s.timeConstrained,
    });
  });
  return out;
}

function paceLabel(p) {
  if (!isFinite(p) || p <= 0) return "—";
  let m = Math.floor(p);
  let sec = Math.round((p - m) * 60);
  if (sec === 60) { m += 1; sec = 0; }
  return `${m}:${String(sec).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function RunGraph({ sessions }) {
  const runs = useMemo(() => runSeries(sessions), [sessions]);

  // The target going into each run, replayed through the same function the coach
  // calls, so the line drawn here can never disagree with the plan on screen.
  // The extra value on the end is today's target, which is what the trailing
  // segment past the last dot is showing.
  const targets = useMemo(() => {
    if (runs.length === 0) return [];
    const past = runs.map((r) => suggestCardioTarget(sessions.slice(0, r.index), r.modality).distance);
    past.push(suggestCardioTarget(sessions, runs[runs.length - 1].modality).distance);
    return past;
  }, [runs, sessions]);

  if (runs.length === 0) return null;

  // Two bands, not one plot. Pace lives on an inverted scale, and when it was
  // drawn over the distance dots it read as a trend line through them, which is
  // a different claim entirely. Separated by a hairline, each scale means only
  // what it says.
  const width = 340, height = 120;
  const dTop = 10, dBot = 80;          // distance + target
  const rule = 86;
  const pTop = 94, pBot = 112;         // pace ribbon
  const n = runs.length;
  // One slot per run plus a trailing slot for today's target. Dots sit centred
  // in their slot so each lands on the target segment it was run against.
  const stepX = width / (n + 1);
  const xRun = (i) => +((i + 0.5) * stepX).toFixed(1);

  const dVals = runs.map((r) => r.distance).concat(targets);
  const dMin = Math.min(...dVals), dMax = Math.max(...dVals);
  const dPad = (dMax - dMin) * 0.15 || 0.1;
  const dLo = dMin - dPad, dHi = dMax + dPad;
  const yD = (v) => +(dBot - ((v - dLo) / (dHi - dLo || 1)) * (dBot - dTop)).toFixed(1);

  const pVals = runs.map((r) => r.pace);
  const pMin = Math.min(...pVals), pMax = Math.max(...pVals);
  const pPad = (pMax - pMin) * 0.2 || 0.5;
  const pLo = pMin - pPad, pHi = pMax + pPad;
  // Inverted deliberately: a lower pace is a faster run, so it belongs higher.
  const yP = (v) => +(pTop + ((v - pLo) / (pHi - pLo || 1)) * (pBot - pTop)).toFixed(1);

  let targetPath = `M 0 ${yD(targets[0])}`;
  for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
    const x = +(i * stepX).toFixed(1);
    targetPath += ` L ${x} ${yD(targets[i - 1])} L ${x} ${yD(targets[i])}`;
  }
  targetPath += ` L ${width} ${yD(targets[n])}`;

  const pacePath = runs.map((r, i) => `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"} ${xRun(i)} ${yP(r.pace)}`).join(" ");
  const dotR = Math.max(1.6, Math.min(3.5, stepX * 0.32));

  const last = runs[n - 1];
  const anyShort = runs.some((r) => r.short);

  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: "18px 20px" }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline", marginBottom: 2 }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B" }}>Running</div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 13, color: "var(--activity)", fontWeight: 600 }}>
          {last.distance}mi
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 8 }}>
        Target {targets[n]}mi · last {paceLabel(last.pace)}/mi
      </div>

      <svg width="100%" height={height} viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} preserveAspectRatio="none" style={{ display: "block" }}>
        <path d={targetPath} fill="none" stroke="var(--activity)" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        {runs.map((r, i) => (
          <circle key={i} cx={xRun(i)} cy={yD(r.distance)} r={r.short ? dotR - 0.7 : dotR}
            fill={r.short ? "#F9F6EE" : "#1B1B1B"}
            stroke={r.short ? "#A9A192" : "#1B1B1B"} strokeWidth="1.5" />
        ))}
        <line x1="0" y1={rule} x2={width} y2={rule} stroke="#D8D0BF" strokeWidth="1" />
        <path d={pacePath} fill="none" stroke="#8C8375" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeDasharray="3 2.5" strokeLinejoin="round" />
      </svg>

      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, flexWrap: "wrap", marginTop: 8, fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 0.5, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>
        <span style={{ color: "#1B1B1B" }}>● run</span>
        {anyShort && <span style={{ color: "#A9A192" }}>○ short on time</span>}
        <span style={{ color: "var(--activity)" }}>▬ target</span>
        <span>┄ pace · faster up</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ---------- practice (daily meditation + journal) ----------
// ---------- how the mind was (non-ranked) ----------
// Replaces the old restless -> absorbed scale, which ranked sits by depth of
// absorption and so quietly made "absorbed" the goal. These two readings are
// diagnostic instead of ordinal: there is no best answer to either. Energy is the
// classical energy/concentration balance, which tells you which awakening factors
// to lean on next time; attitude is the observing mind's own stance.
const MIND_ENERGY = [
  { key: "slack", short: "Slack", label: "Slack — dull, sinking, low energy" },
  { key: "balanced", short: "Balanced", label: "Balanced — energy and steadiness met" },
  { key: "agitated", short: "Agitated", label: "Agitated — restless, pushing, busy" },
];

const MIND_ATTITUDE = [
  { key: "allowing", short: "Allowing", label: "Allowing — letting it be as it was" },
  { key: "wanting", short: "Wanting", label: "Wanting — after a state, a depth, a result" },
  { key: "resisting", short: "Resisting", label: "Resisting — pushing something away" },
  { key: "unclear", short: "Unclear", label: "Hard to tell" },
];

// Entries logged before this change carry a `rating` from the old scale. They stay
// exactly as they were and still render — nothing in the log is rewritten.
function practiceReadout(p) {
  const parts = [];
  const t = MEDITATION_TYPES.find((x) => x.key === p.type);
  if (t) parts.push(t.label);
  const e = MIND_ENERGY.find((x) => x.key === p.energy);
  const a = MIND_ATTITUDE.find((x) => x.key === p.attitude);
  if (e) parts.push(e.short);
  if (a) parts.push(a.short);
  if (!e && !a) {
    const legacy = MEDITATION_RATINGS.find((x) => x.key === p.rating);
    if (legacy) parts.push(legacy.label);
  }
  return parts.join(" · ");
}

// Last few readings, side by side, shown while the current one is being chosen.
// Deliberately just the readings: no counts, no majority, no "you've been
// agitated lately". Two entries is enough for this to be worth looking at, and
// it gets more informative on its own as the log fills, without ever needing to
// claim a pattern.
function RecentSits({ practice, limit = 5 }) {
  const recent = useMemo(
    () => [...(practice || [])]
      .filter((p) => p.energy || p.attitude)
      .sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.date !== b.date) return (a.date || "") < (b.date || "") ? 1 : -1;
        return (b.id || 0) - (a.id || 0);
      })
      .slice(0, limit),
    [practice, limit]
  );
  if (recent.length === 0) return null;
  const MONTHS = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
  const dayLabel = (ds) => {
    if (!ds || ds.length < 10) return "—";
    const m = MONTHS[Number(ds.slice(5, 7)) - 1];
    return m ? `${m} ${Number(ds.slice(8, 10))}` : "—";
  };
  const shortOf = (list, key) => {
    const hit = list.find((x) => x.key === key);
    return hit ? hit.short : "—";
  };
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "2px solid #D8D0BF", background: "#EFE9DC", padding: "10px 12px" }}>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 8 }}>
        {recent.length === 1 ? "Last sit" : `Last ${recent.length} sits`}
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${recent.length}, 1fr)`, gap: 0 }}>
        {recent.map((p, i) => (
          <div key={p.id || i} style={{
            paddingLeft: i === 0 ? 0 : 8,
            borderLeft: i === 0 ? "none" : "1px solid #D8D0BF",
          }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 9.5, color: "#6E6759", letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
              {dayLabel(p.date)}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 4, lineHeight: 1.3 }}>
              {shortOf(MIND_ENERGY, p.energy)}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 1, lineHeight: 1.3 }}>
              {shortOf(MIND_ATTITUDE, p.attitude)}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.5, marginTop: 8 }}>
        Context, not comparison — there's no better answer on either row.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function MindStep({ entry, setEntry, practice }) {
  return (
    <Card title="How was the mind?" sub="Two readings, neither one a score">
      <Field label="Energy">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
          {MIND_ENERGY.map((r) => (
            <Chip key={r.key} active={entry.energy === r.key} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, energy: r.key }))}>{r.label}</Chip>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Field>
      <Field label="Attitude of the observing mind">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
          {MIND_ATTITUDE.map((r) => (
            <Chip key={r.key} active={entry.attitude === r.key} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, attitude: r.key }))}>{r.label}</Chip>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Field>
      <RecentSits practice={practice} />
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.6, marginTop: 4 }}>
        Slack asks for interest and energy next time; agitated asks for settling. Neither is a better sit.
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function ReviewLine({ entry }) {
  const e = MIND_ENERGY.find((x) => x.key === entry.energy);
  const a = MIND_ATTITUDE.find((x) => x.key === entry.attitude);
  return (
    <div>{e ? e.short : "No energy noted"} · {a ? a.short : "No attitude noted"}</div>
  );
}

function PracticeWizard({ onCancel, onComplete, practice }) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [entry, setEntry] = useState({
    date: localDateStr(),
    type: null, duration: 15, energy: null, attitude: null, journal: "",
  });
  const steps = ["type", "duration", "mind", "journal", "review"];
  const total = steps.length;
  const current = steps[step];
  const next = () => setStep((s) => Math.min(total - 1, s + 1));
  const back = () => setStep((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1));
  const finish = () => onComplete({ id: uid(), ...entry });

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 480, margin: "0 auto" }}>
      <StepDots step={step} total={total} />

      {current === "type" && (
        <Card title="Practice" sub="What kind of sit was it?">
          <Field label="Date">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, justifyContent: "center", flexWrap: "wrap", marginBottom: 10 }}>
              {[0, 1, 2].map((back) => {
                const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() - back);
                const ds = localDateStr(d);
                return (
                  <Chip key={back} active={entry.date === ds} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, date: ds }))}>
                    {back === 0 ? "Today" : back === 1 ? "Yesterday" : "2 days ago"}
                  </Chip>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <input
              type="date"
              value={entry.date}
              max={localDateStr()}
              onChange={(e) => e.target.value && setEntry((prev) => ({ ...prev, date: e.target.value }))}
              style={{
                width: "100%", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 12px",
                fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 14, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
              }}
            />
          </Field>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
            {MEDITATION_TYPES.map((t) => (
              <Chip key={t.key} active={entry.type === t.key} onClick={() => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, type: t.key }))}>{t.label}</Chip>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {current === "duration" && (
        <Card title="Duration" sub="How long did you sit?">
          <Stepper value={entry.duration} step={5} unit="min" min={1} onChange={(v) => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, duration: v }))} />
        </Card>
      )}

      {current === "mind" && (
        <MindStep entry={entry} setEntry={setEntry} practice={practice} />
      )}

      {current === "journal" && (
        <Card title="Journal" sub="Optional — whatever's worth writing down">
          <PointerPrompt type={entry.type} />
          <SilaJournalPrompt />
          <textarea
            value={entry.journal}
            onChange={(ev) => setEntry((e) => ({ ...e, journal: ev.target.value }))}
            placeholder="What came up, what you noticed, anything on your mind…"
            style={{
              width: "100%", minHeight: 140, borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B",
              padding: 12, fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 14, resize: "vertical",
              background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6,
            }}
          />
        </Card>
      )}

      {current === "review" && (
        <Card title="Review & save">
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.9 }}>
            <div>{MEDITATION_TYPES.find((t) => t.key === entry.type)?.label || "No type set"} · {entry.duration} min</div>
            <ReviewLine entry={entry} />
            {entry.journal && <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontStyle: "italic" }}>"{entry.journal.slice(0, 120)}{entry.journal.length > 120 ? "…" : ""}"</div>}
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginTop: 22 }}>
        <button style={btnGhost} onClick={step === 0 ? onCancel : back}>
          <ChevronLeft size={16} /> {step === 0 ? "Cancel" : "Back"}
        </button>
        {current === "review" ? (
          <button style={btnPrimary} onClick={finish}><Check size={16} /> Save entry</button>
        ) : (
          <button style={btnPrimary} onClick={next}>Next <ChevronRight size={16} /></button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
