function pointerPool(type) {
  // Imported passages lead each pool; the built-in prompts sit underneath until
  // enough have been imported for that slot to stand on its own.
  const general = blendedPool(importedGeneral(), PRACTICE_POINTERS.filter((p) => !p.types), LIBRARY_FULL.general);
  if (!type) return general;
  const matched = blendedPool(importedForType(type), PRACTICE_POINTERS.filter((p) => p.types && p.types.indexOf(type) !== -1), LIBRARY_FULL.type);
  // Type-specific pointers are few, so fall back to the general pool rather
  // than showing the same handful every time he picks that practice.
  return matched.length ? matched : general;
}

function pointerForDate(dateStr, pool, offset) {
  const n = pool.length;
  if (n === 0) return null;
  const days = Math.floor(Date.parse(dateStr + "T00:00:00") / 86400000);
  if (!Number.isFinite(days)) return pool[0];
  const gcd = (a, b) => (b === 0 ? a : gcd(b, a % b));
  let stride = 17;
  while (gcd(stride, n) !== 1) stride += 1;
  return pool[(((days * stride) % n) + n + (offset || 0)) % n];
}

// ---------- kitchen rules (the cook's own knowledge base) ----------
// These used to be hard-coded into the chef prompt, which meant every lesson
// learned at the stove cost a rebuild and an upload to write down. They live in
// storage now: the seeded ones are the constraints this kitchen already proved,
// and anything added later carries exactly the same weight. The chef assembles
// its constraints from this list at prompt time.
//
// A rule is not a diary entry. Twenty sharp constraints make the chef sharper;
// two hundred observations make it worse and bury the ones that matter, which
// is why the editor pushes toward instructions and makes pruning easy.
const SEED_RULES = [
  { id: "r-oven", text: "The oven runs hot. Always 400°F on the middle rack, and give a target internal temperature for any protein rather than relying on time.", why: "Timings from recipes overshoot in this oven.", seeded: true },
  { id: "r-greens", text: "Use kale (massaged, lacinato) for anything that has to last five days. Spinach dies by day 3-4 — only use it if you say so explicitly.", why: "Learned from prep batches going slimy midweek.", seeded: true },
  { id: "r-cilantro", text: "Cilantro is a finishing herb only. Never cooked in.", why: "", seeded: true },
  { id: "r-cumin", text: "Cumin: 1 to 1.5 tsp maximum, ever.", why: "It has ruined a batch here before — needed yogurt, lemon and cucumber to rescue it.", seeded: true },
  { id: "r-cannedfish", text: "Canned fish did not land here. The texture is the problem, not the seasoning. Do not build a dish around canned salmon or canned tuna unless explicitly asked for it.", why: "The canned salmon bowls: dressing was a keeper, the fish was not.", seeded: true },
  { id: "r-rotisserie", text: "Rotisserie chicken is a legitimate and preferred shortcut for any chicken recipe.", why: "A batch failed on under-thawed thighs; the rotisserie rebuild was excellent.", seeded: true },
  { id: "r-equipment", text: "Equipment available: Zojirushi rice cooker, foil-lined sheet pans, standard pots and pans. No sous vide or specialty gear.", why: "", seeded: true },
  { id: "r-shopping", text: "Shopping happens in western Massachusetts near Leverett — Big Y, Market Basket, sometimes Food City. Ordinary New England supermarkets, not specialty grocers. If a dish really wants an Asian-market or Middle Eastern ingredient, name the supermarket substitute instead and say what was swapped.", why: "", seeded: true },
];

// Seeded rules can be edited or deleted like any other — the merge only fills in
// ones that have never been seen, so a rule deliberately removed stays removed
// instead of reappearing at every launch.
function mergeRules(stored) {
  if (!Array.isArray(stored)) return SEED_RULES;
  return stored.filter((r) => r && typeof r.text === "string" && r.text.trim());
}

function rulesBlock(rules) {
  const list = (rules || []).filter((r) => r && (r.text || "").trim());
  if (!list.length) return "";
  return "NON-NEGOTIABLE CONSTRAINTS — this kitchen has taught these the hard way:\n" +
    list.map((r) => `- ${r.text.trim()}${(r.why || "").trim() ? ` (${r.why.trim()})` : ""}`).join("\n");
}

// Freewriting is the point: the cook should be able to dump what happened at the
// stove without composing an instruction. This turns that into a rule the chef
// can actually obey, and hands it back for editing rather than saving it blind.
const RULE_DISTILL_SYSTEM = `You turn a home cook's rough notes into a single durable kitchen rule for a recipe-generating chef.

The note describes something learned while cooking. Convert it into ONE instruction the chef must follow in future recipes. Requirements:
- Write the rule as a directive, addressed to the chef, in the imperative. Not a story, not a past-tense report.
- Keep it to one or two sentences. Be specific: quantities, temperatures, ingredient names, and timings if the note gives them.
- Do not invent detail the note does not contain. If the note is vague, keep the rule correspondingly broad rather than making something up.
- If the note contains more than one distinct lesson, pick the single most useful one and put the rest aside.
- "why" is a short phrase giving the reason or the incident behind it, in the cook's own terms. Leave it empty if the note gives no reason.

Respond with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fence, no preamble:
{"text":"","why":""}`;

// ---------- activity colour ----------
// One accent carries everything that is the cook's own doing: the weight
// sparkline, the minutes sat, the frame around a recipe being brought in, and
// the handwriting in the annotation style. Links out are not that — going to
// someone else's site is ink — and neither is rejection, which keeps its own
// red.
//
// Ember, chosen off the trial. It sits in the same earth-pigment family as the
// rest of the palette, which the old #0043FF never did: that blue was the only
// colour here that couldn't exist as ink on paper. It is the closest of the
// candidates to the rejection red at #BE3B2A, so the two are kept apart by
// form as well as hue — activity appears as strokes, rules and small fills,
// rejection as bordered panels.
//
// It stays behind a custom property rather than being pasted into 48 style
// objects, so the next change to it is one line.
const ACTIVITY_COLOR = "#C0632A";
const ACTIVITY_TINT = "#F7E7D9";

function applyTheme() {
  try {
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--activity", ACTIVITY_COLOR);
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--activity-tint", ACTIVITY_TINT);
  } catch (e) {}
}


// ---------- sila period (annual training period) ----------
// Two months given over to the training precepts, September 1 to October 31,
// every year. It is not a streak and there is nothing to score. The period does
// three things: it names itself, it keeps the five precepts in view, and it
// marks its own two edges — the day it opens and the day it closes. Both edges
// are undertaken by hand rather than arriving automatically, because the point
// of a marker is the deliberate act of making it.
const SILA_START_MONTH = 9;   // September
const SILA_START_DAY = 1;
const SILA_END_MONTH = 10;    // October
const SILA_END_DAY = 31;

const PRECEPTS = [
  {
    n: 1,
    short: "Harming",
    text: "To refrain from killing or harming living beings.",
    gloss: "Care for what is alive, including the small and the inconvenient.",
  },
  {
    n: 2,
    short: "Taking",
    text: "To refrain from taking what is not given.",
    gloss: "Not only property. Time, credit and attention can all be taken without being offered.",
  },
  {
    n: 3,
    short: "Sexuality",
    text: "To refrain from sexual misconduct.",
    gloss: "Not causing harm through sexuality; keeping faith with what has been promised.",
  },
  {
    n: 4,
    short: "Speech",
    text: "To refrain from false speech.",
    gloss: "The plain lie, and also the exaggeration, the convenient omission, the version that flatters.",
  },
  {
    n: 5,
    short: "Heedlessness",
    text: "To refrain from intoxicants that cloud the mind.",
    gloss: "The one that guards the other four: clouded, they all get harder to keep.",
  },
];

// A separate subpool, kept out of PRACTICE_POINTERS so it can be weighted
// differently. Each pointer names the precept it belongs to, which is what the
// Today band highlights — precept 0 means the training as a whole.
const SILA_POINTERS = [

// Is this date inside the period, and where in it? Returns null outside.
function silaPeriod(dateStr) {
  if (!dateStr || dateStr.length < 10) return null;
  const year = Number(dateStr.slice(0, 4));
  if (!Number.isFinite(year)) return null;
  const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  const start = `${year}-${pad(SILA_START_MONTH)}-${pad(SILA_START_DAY)}`;
  const end = `${year}-${pad(SILA_END_MONTH)}-${pad(SILA_END_DAY)}`;
  if (dateStr < start || dateStr > end) return null;
  const DAY = 86400000;
  const t0 = Date.parse(start + "T00:00:00");
  const t1 = Date.parse(end + "T00:00:00");
  const tn = Date.parse(dateStr + "T00:00:00");
  if (!Number.isFinite(t0) || !Number.isFinite(tn)) return null;
  const dayIndex = Math.round((tn - t0) / DAY);
  return {
    year, start, end, dayIndex,
    dayNumber: dayIndex + 1,
    totalDays: Math.round((t1 - t0) / DAY) + 1,
    isOpeningDay: dayIndex === 0,
    isClosingDay: dateStr === end,
  };
}

// During the period the daily pointer leans hard on the precepts without
// crowding the rest out: two days in three come from the sila subpool, the
// third from the general library, so insight practice stays interwoven rather
// than being suspended for two months.
// Same coprime-stride walk as pointerForDate, but driven by a caller-supplied
// ordinal instead of the raw date.
function pointerFromPool(pool, ordinal) {
  const n = pool.length;
  if (n === 0) return null;
  const gcd = (a, b) => (b === 0 ? a : gcd(b, a % b));
  let stride = 17;
  while (gcd(stride, n) !== 1) stride += 1;
  return pool[(((ordinal * stride) % n) + n) % n];
}

function dailyPointerFor(dateStr, type, offset) {
  const period = silaPeriod(dateStr);
  const off = offset || 0;
  if (period && ((period.dayIndex + off) % 3) !== 2) {
    // Count sila days rather than calendar days. Only two days in three land
    // here, and a date-driven index walking a fixed stride can never reach a
    // third of the pool once that pattern is imposed on it — eight pointers
    // would simply never appear. The ordinal counts the days that actually use
    // this pool, and the year term keeps next September from repeating this one.
    const d = period.dayIndex + off;
    const ordinal = Math.floor(d / 3) * 2 + (d % 3) + period.year * 11;
    const p = pointerFromPool(silaPool(), ordinal);
    if (p) return p;
  }
  if (period) {
    // The interwoven third used to index by date, which only held while the
    // general pool's length happened not to be a multiple of 3. The pool grows
    // with every import now, so that assumption can't be relied on: this day
    // gets the same ordinal treatment, counting the days that actually draw
    // from this pool rather than the calendar.
    const d = period.dayIndex + off;
    const p = pointerFromPool(pointerPool(type), Math.floor(d / 3) + period.year * 5);
    if (p) return p;
  }
  return pointerForDate(dateStr, pointerPool(type), off);
}

// The precept the band highlights. When today's pointer is a sila one it names
// its own precept, so the highlight and the day's inspiration agree. On the
// interwoven days the pointer is a general one, and the precepts simply rotate.
function preceptOfDay(dateStr) {
  const period = silaPeriod(dateStr);
  if (!period) return null;
  const p = dailyPointerFor(dateStr, null, 0);
  if (p && p.precept) return p.precept;
  return (period.dayIndex % PRECEPTS.length) + 1;
}

// ---------- imported teachings ----------
// The built-in pointer library is written in this app's own voice — useful, but
// not the teaching itself. A teaching imported from a URL is stored whole, in
// reading order, in the teacher's own words, and the daily pointer draws its
// lines from that. The built-in pool stays underneath as a floor so the Practice
// tab is never blank, and retires slot by slot as the imported library fills.
const TEACHINGS_KEY = "teachings";

// A dharma talk transcript runs 3–6k words. The cap is generous enough for a
// long sutta and low enough that one bad import can't eat the storage quota
// every other feature shares.
const TEACHING_CHAR_CAP = 80000;

// Counts at which the built-in pointers stop being mixed in. Held per slot,
// because the type-matched journal prompts fill far more slowly than the
// general daily pointer does.
const LIBRARY_FULL = { general: 20, type: 8, sila: 15 };

// Pool functions are called from components scattered across the tree — the
// daily pointer, the journal prompt inside the wizard, the sila prompt — and
// threading a teachings prop through all of them would mean touching every
// component in between. The list is mirrored here instead and refreshed
// whenever the state that owns it changes, so a re-render always reads current
// data. App state remains the single source of truth; this is a read cache.
let IMPORTED_TEACHINGS: any[] = [];
function setImportedTeachings(list) {
  IMPORTED_TEACHINGS = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
}

// Flatten every teaching's pointer-worthy segments into one list, each carrying
// its own attribution so a line can always be traced back to who said it.
function teachingPassages() {
  const out: any[] = [];
  for (const t of IMPORTED_TEACHINGS) {
    const segs = (t && t.segments) || [];
    for (let i = 0; i < segs.length; i++) {
      const s = segs[i];
      if (!s || !s.pointer || !s.text) continue;
      out.push({
        theme: s.theme || "Teaching",
        text: s.text,
        source: [t.author, t.work || t.title].filter(Boolean).join(" \u2014 "),
        url: t.url || null,
        types: s.types,
        precept: s.precept,
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function importedGeneral() {
  return teachingPassages().filter((p) => !p.types && p.precept === undefined);
}
function importedForType(type) {
  return teachingPassages().filter((p) => p.types && p.types.indexOf(type) !== -1);
}
function importedSila() {
  return teachingPassages().filter((p) => p.precept !== undefined);
}

// Below the threshold the two pools are shown together, so an imported line
// starts appearing the day it arrives instead of waiting for a quota. At or
// above it the built-ins drop out of that slot entirely.
function blendedPool(imported, builtin, threshold) {
  return imported.length >= threshold ? imported : imported.concat(builtin);
}

function silaPool() {
  return blendedPool(importedSila(), SILA_POINTERS, LIBRARY_FULL.sila);
}

// How full each slot is, for the readout on the Practice tab.
function libraryStatus() {
  return {
    sources: IMPORTED_TEACHINGS.length,
    passages: teachingPassages().length,
    generalFull: importedGeneral().length >= LIBRARY_FULL.general,
    silaFull: importedSila().length >= LIBRARY_FULL.sila,
  };
}

// ---------- importing ----------
const TEACHING_SYSTEM = `You are importing a Buddhist teaching into a practitioner's personal practice journal. You are an extractor, not an author.

Absolute rules:
- Every word you place in "segments" must be the teacher's own words, copied verbatim from the page. Do not paraphrase, summarise, modernise, condense or compose. If you cannot copy it, leave it out.
- Add no commentary, framing or pointers of your own. Not one sentence.
- Skip navigation, headers, footers, donation appeals, comment threads, copyright notices and audio player text.
- Some pages return JSON rather than prose. Rebuild the teaching from it before segmenting, and never present JSON keys, segment ids or markup as text.
- If what comes back holds under roughly 1000 characters of actual teaching — an empty page shell, a page whose talk is audio only, a bare index or a scan with no text layer — return {"error":"too short"} and nothing else. Do not reconstruct, summarise or fill in a page that did not come back.

Return ONLY a JSON object. No preamble, no markdown fences:
{
  "title": "the title as the page gives it",
  "author": "the teacher, or the translator, as the page names them",
  "work": "sutta reference or collection if the page gives one, otherwise null",
  "segments": [
    { "text": "verbatim text", "pointer": true, "theme": "Attitude", "types": ["breath"], "precept": 4 }
  ]
}

The segments must cover the whole teaching in reading order, so that joining them back together reproduces it. Break at paragraph boundaries. Keep each segment under roughly 90 words, splitting a long paragraph at sentence boundaries.

For each segment:
- "pointer": true only when the segment stands on its own as something worth holding in mind during a sit. Narrative setup, retreat logistics, asides and sentences that need the previous one to make sense are false. Expect roughly a quarter to a third to be true.
- "theme": two or three words naming what it concerns — Attitude, Effort, Hindrances, Metta, Impermanence and so on. Only on segments where pointer is true.
- "types": only when the segment speaks to one specific practice. Allowed: "breath", "bodyScan", "metta", "open". Omit otherwise.
- "precept": only when the segment concerns ethical conduct. 1 harming, 2 taking what is not given, 3 sexual misconduct, 4 false speech, 5 intoxicants, 0 for virtue in general. Omit otherwise.

If the page is readable but is not a Buddhist teaching — an index, a biography, a shop page — return {"error":"not a teaching"}.`;

// SuttaCentral renders its text in the browser, so fetching the page itself
// returns an empty shell that used to arrive as a garbled or misleading import.
// Its text lives behind a JSON API instead, so those links get rewritten before
// anything is fetched.
function suttaCentralUid(url) {
  const m = /^https?:\/\/(?:www\.)?suttacentral\.net\/([a-z]+[0-9][a-z0-9.\-]*)/i.exec((url || "").trim());
  return m ? m[1].toLowerCase() : null;
}

// Below this, whatever came back isn't a talk: an app shell, an audio-only page
// or a scanned PDF with no text layer all land here. Gating on length is what
// keeps those from being segmented into nonsense and stored as a teaching.
const TEACHING_MIN_CHARS = 1000;

function teachingRequest(url) {
  const uid = suttaCentralUid(url);
  if (!uid) return "Import the teaching at " + url;
  return [
    "Import the teaching at " + url + ".",
    "",
    "That page renders its text in the browser, so fetching it returns an empty shell. Fetch these instead, in order, stopping at the first that returns the text:",
    "",
    "1. https://suttacentral.net/api/bilarasuttas/" + uid + "/sujato",
    "   A JSON object. The translation sits in \"translation_text\", keyed by segment id. Join those values in the order given by \"keys_order\" (or the object's own key order if that is absent), skipping empty strings, to rebuild the text. Segment ids are not part of the teaching.",
    "2. https://suttacentral.net/api/extractsutta/" + uid + "/sujato",
    "   Use this if the first returns nothing usable, as it does for range suttas.",
    "3. The published Bilara data on raw.githubusercontent.com, under suttacentral/bilara-data/published/translation/en/sujato/sutta/, in the folder for that collection — mn8 sits at .../mn/mn8_translation-en-sujato.json, sn12.23 at .../sn/sn12/sn12.23_translation-en-sujato.json. Use this if the first two return a 500.",
    "",
    "Title, translator and sutta reference come from that data where present; otherwise take the reference from the id " + uid + " and the translator as Bhikkhu Sujato.",
  ].join("\n");
}

async function fetchTeaching(url) {
  const key = getApiKey();
  if (!key) throw new Error("no-key");
  const call = (messages) =>
    fetch("https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "x-api-key": key,
        "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
        "anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access": "true",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: "claude-sonnet-4-6",
        // The whole talk comes back segmented, so the reply is about as long as
        // the talk itself. A 4,000-word transcript lands near 12k tokens.
        max_tokens: 16000,
        system: TEACHING_SYSTEM,
        tools: [{
          type: "web_fetch_20250910",
          name: "web_fetch",
          max_uses: 4,
          max_content_tokens: 40000,
        }],
        messages,
      }),
    });

  let messages: any[] = [{ role: "user", content: teachingRequest(url) }];
  let blocks: any[] = [];
  let stop: any = null;
  // Same pause_turn handling as the recipe import: a server-side fetch can
  // return early expecting the conversation handed straight back.
  for (let turn = 0; turn < 3; turn++) {
    const response = await call(messages);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`API error ${response.status}`);
    const data = await response.json();
    blocks = blocks.concat(data.content || []);
    stop = data.stop_reason;
    if (stop === "pause_turn") {
      messages = messages.concat([{ role: "assistant", content: data.content }]);
      continue;
    }
    break;
  }
  const fetchError = blocks
    .filter((c) => c.type === "web_fetch_tool_result")
    .map((c) => c.content && c.content.error_code)
    .find(Boolean);
  if (fetchError) throw new Error("fetch:" + fetchError);

  const text = blocks.filter((c) => c.type === "text").map((c) => c.text).join("");
  const cleaned = text.replace(/```json/g, "").replace(/```/g, "").trim();
  const first = cleaned.indexOf("{");
  const last = cleaned.lastIndexOf("}");
  if (first < 0 || last < 0) throw new Error(stop === "max_tokens" ? "truncated" : "unreadable");
  let parsed;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(cleaned.slice(first, last + 1));
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error(stop === "max_tokens" ? "truncated" : "unreadable");
  }
  if (parsed && parsed.error) {
    throw new Error(String(parsed.error).indexOf("short") !== -1 ? "too-short" : "not-a-teaching");
  }

  const segsIn = Array.isArray(parsed.segments) ? parsed.segments : [];
  // Trust nothing about the shape coming back. A stray precept of 9 or a types
  // value the app has never heard of would sit in storage forever and quietly
  // route a passage into a pool that never surfaces it.
  const TYPES = ["breath", "bodyScan", "metta", "open"];
  const segments: any[] = [];
  let chars = 0;
  for (const s of segsIn) {
    const t = typeof s?.text === "string" ? s.text.trim() : "";
    if (!t) continue;
    chars += t.length;
    if (chars > TEACHING_CHAR_CAP) break;
    const seg: any = { text: t, pointer: s.pointer === true };
    if (seg.pointer && typeof s.theme === "string" && s.theme.trim()) seg.theme = s.theme.trim();
    const types = Array.isArray(s.types) ? s.types.filter((x) => TYPES.indexOf(x) !== -1) : [];
    if (types.length) seg.types = types;
    if (typeof s.precept === "number" && s.precept >= 0 && s.precept <= 5) seg.precept = s.precept;
    segments.push(seg);
  }
  if (!segments.length) throw new Error("empty");
  // Second gate, on what actually arrived rather than on what the reader said
  // about it. An app shell can still produce two or three stray segments.
  if (chars < TEACHING_MIN_CHARS) throw new Error("too-short");

  return {
    id: Date.now(),
    url,
    title: (typeof parsed.title === "string" && parsed.title.trim()) || url,
    author: (typeof parsed.author === "string" && parsed.author.trim()) || "Unattributed",
    work: (typeof parsed.work === "string" && parsed.work.trim()) || null,
    addedAt: localDateStr(),
    truncated: chars > TEACHING_CHAR_CAP,
    segments,
  };
}

const TEACHING_ERRORS = {
  "no-key": "No API key saved yet \u2014 add one under Plan \u00B7 Settings first.",
  "not-a-teaching": "That page read fine, but it's an index, a biography or a listing rather than a talk. Open the talk itself and use that link.",
  "too-short": "Almost no readable text came off that page. Audio-only talks, PDF-only sites and pages that build themselves in the browser all arrive empty. Look for a page with the transcript written out as text.",
  truncated: "That talk came back longer than there was room for. Try a single talk rather than a whole collection page.",
  unreadable: "The reply came back garbled. Worth trying once more.",
  empty: "Nothing readable came off that page \u2014 most likely audio-only or a PDF.",
};

// ---------- the teachings card on the Practice tab ----------
function TeachingsCard({ teachings, setTeachings }) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState("");
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState(null);
  const [confirmId, setConfirmId] = useState(null);
  const status = libraryStatus();

  const runImport = async () => {
    const u = url.trim();
    if (!u) return;
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(u)) { setErr("That needs to start with http:// or https://"); return; }
    if (teachings.some((t) => t.url === u)) { setErr("That link is already in the library."); return; }
    setBusy(true); setErr(""); setNote("");
    try {
      const t = await fetchTeaching(u);
      setTeachings((prev) => [t, ...prev]);
      setUrl("");
      const kept = t.segments.filter((s) => s.pointer).length;
      setNote(`Added "${t.title}" \u2014 ${t.segments.length} passages, ${kept} in the rotation.`);
    } catch (e: any) {
      const m = String(e && e.message ? e.message : e);
      setErr(
        m.slice(0, 6) === "fetch:"
          ? (FETCH_ERRORS[m.slice(6)] || "That page couldn't be read.")
          : (TEACHING_ERRORS[m] || "That import didn't go through. Worth trying once more.")
      );
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const remove = (id) => {
    setTeachings((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
    setConfirmId(null);
    if (openId === id) setOpenId(null);
  };

  const mono = { fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace" };

  // Collapsed by default. This is a library, not a daily reading — the tab it
  // sits on is for logging a sit, and a growing list of talks would push that
  // below the fold within a fortnight.
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0 }}>
      <div
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        style={{ padding: "16px 18px", cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}
      >
        <div>
          <div style={{ ...mono, fontSize: 11, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>Teachings</div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.8, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 2 }}>
            {status.passages} passage{status.passages === 1 ? "" : "s"}
          </div>
          <div style={{ ...mono, fontSize: 10.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 3 }}>
            {status.sources} SOURCE{status.sources === 1 ? "" : "S"} · {status.generalFull ? "LIBRARY CARRYING" : "BUILT-INS FILLING IN"}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 800, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1 }}>{open ? "\u2013" : "+"}</div>
      </div>

      {open && (
      <div style={{ padding: "0 18px 18px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>
      <div>
        <input
          value={url}
          onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="https://www.accesstoinsight.org/..."
          style={{ width: "100%", boxSizing: "border-box", borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 12px", fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12.5, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B" }}
        />
        <button
          onClick={runImport}
          disabled={busy}
          style={{ ...btnPrimary, marginTop: 10, width: "100%", justifyContent: "center", opacity: busy ? 0.55 : 1 }}
        >
          {busy ? "Reading the page\u2026" : "Import teaching"}
        </button>
        {err && (
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#BE3B2A", marginTop: 10, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>{err}</div>
        )}
        {note && !err && (
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#2F6B4F", marginTop: 10, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>{note}</div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.6, borderTop: "2px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 12 }}>
        {status.generalFull
          ? "The library carries the daily pointer on its own now \u2014 the built-in prompts have stepped back."
          : `Built-in prompts are still filling the gaps. ${Math.max(0, LIBRARY_FULL.general - importedGeneral().length)} more imported passages and they retire.`}
      </div>

      {teachings.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
          Nothing imported yet. accesstoinsight.org, dhammatalks.org, ajahnchah.org, plumvillage.org transcriptions and insightmeditationcenter.org transcribed talks all read cleanly, and suttacentral.net links are rewritten to its text API. Audio-only talks and PDF-only sites — Forest Sangha, Amaravati, audiodharma — have no text to import.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
          {teachings.map((t) => {
            const open = openId === t.id;
            const kept = t.segments.filter((s) => s.pointer).length;
            return (
              <div key={t.id} style={{ border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", background: "#F9F6EE", padding: "12px 14px" }}>
                <div
                  onClick={() => setOpenId(open ? null : t.id)}
                  style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}
                >
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.4 }}>{t.title}</div>
                  <div style={{ ...mono, fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 4 }}>
                    {t.author}{t.work ? " \u00B7 " + t.work : ""}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ ...mono, fontSize: 10.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 3 }}>
                    {t.segments.length} PASSAGES · {kept} IN ROTATION · ADDED {t.addedAt}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {open && (
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 12, borderTop: "2px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 12 }}>
                    <div style={{ maxHeight: 340, overflowY: "auto", paddingRight: 4 }}>
                      {t.segments.map((s, i) => (
                        <div
                          key={i}
                          style={{
                            fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13.5, lineHeight: 1.65,
                            color: s.pointer ? "#1B1B1B" : "#6E6759",
                            borderLeft: s.pointer ? "3px solid #C0632A" : "3px solid transparent",
                            paddingLeft: 10, marginBottom: 10,
                          }}
                        >
                          {s.text}
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    {t.truncated && (
                      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#BE3B2A", marginTop: 6 }}>
                        This one was long enough to be cut short. The stored part is above.
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 12, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                      {t.url && (
                        <a href={t.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{ ...btnGhost, textDecoration: "none", fontSize: 13, padding: "8px 14px" }}>
                          Open source
                        </a>
                      )}
                      {confirmId === t.id ? (
                        <>
                          <button onClick={() => remove(t.id)} style={{ ...btnGhost, fontSize: 13, padding: "8px 14px", borderColor: "#BE3B2A", color: "#BE3B2A" }}>
                            Remove for good
                          </button>
                          <button onClick={() => setConfirmId(null)} style={{ ...btnGhost, fontSize: 13, padding: "8px 14px" }}>Keep</button>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <button onClick={() => setConfirmId(t.id)} style={{ ...btnGhost, fontSize: 13, padding: "8px 14px", color: "#6E6759", borderColor: "#D8D0BF" }}>
                          Remove
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
      </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// The sila journal prompt used to close with a question written in this app's
// voice. When the library holds something a teacher actually said about the
// precept in view, that goes there instead, and the written question drops back
// to being the floor.
function preceptPassage(dateStr, n) {
  const pool = importedSila().filter((p) => p.precept === n || p.precept === 0);
  if (!pool.length) return null;
  const days = Math.floor(Date.parse(dateStr + "T00:00:00") / 86400000);
  return pointerFromPool(pool, Number.isFinite(days) ? days : 0);
}

function DailyPointer() {
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0);
  const p = dailyPointerFor(localDateStr(), null, offset);
  if (!p) return null;
  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: "16px 18px" }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>{p.theme}</div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setOffset((o) => o + 1)}
          title="Show another pointer"
          style={{
            border: "2px solid #D8D0BF", background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#6E6759", borderRadius: 0,
            cursor: "pointer", padding: "3px 9px", flex: "0 0 auto",
            fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: 0.8, textTransform: "uppercase",
          }}
        >
          Another
        </button>
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 1.65, color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 10 }}>{p.text}</div>
      {(p.source || p.url) && (
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 10 }}>
          {p.source}
          {p.url && (
            <a href={p.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{ color: "#6E6759", marginLeft: p.source ? 8 : 0 }}>Source</a>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function PointerPrompt({ type }) {
  const p = pointerForDate(localDateStr(), pointerPool(type), 0);
  if (!p) return null;
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "2px dotted #A9A192", background: "#EFE9DC", padding: "10px 12px", marginBottom: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 6 }}>Worth considering</div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13.5, lineHeight: 1.6, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{p.text}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PracticeTab({ practice, onStartLog, onUpdateEntry, onDeleteEntry, teachings = [], setTeachings }) {
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null);
  const [confirmId, setConfirmId] = useState(null);

  // Streak deliberately removed: counting consecutive days measures whether you sat,
  // not what happened while sitting, and turns the log into a thing to protect.
  const satToday = useMemo(() => practice.some((p) => p.date === localDateStr()), [practice]);

  // Sort by date rather than relying on insertion order — once a date is editable,
  // the order entries were logged in stops matching the order they happened in.
  // Ties fall back to id (which is Date.now()-based), so two sits on the same day
  // still read newest-logged first, the way the reversed list used to.
  const ordered = useMemo(
    () => [...practice].sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.date !== b.date) return (a.date || "") < (b.date || "") ? 1 : -1;
      return (b.id || 0) - (a.id || 0);
    }),
    [practice]
  );

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>
      <div style={{ background: "#1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: 24, color: "#F9F6EE", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #75695A" }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.6, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>Daily practice</div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 38, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -1.4, textTransform: "uppercase", margin: "4px 0 14px" }}>
          {satToday ? "Sat today" : "Log today's sit"}
        </div>
        <button onClick={onStartLog} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
          Log meditation & journal <ChevronRight size={16} />
        </button>
      </div>

      <DailyPointer />

      <TeachingsCard teachings={teachings} setTeachings={setTeachings} />

      {practice.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#6E6759", textAlign: "center", padding: "20px 0" }}>
          No entries yet — your first sit will start the log.
        </div>
      ) : (
        ordered.map((p) => (
          <div key={p.id} style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: "14px 18px" }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 10 }}>
              <button
                onClick={() => { setEditingId(editingId === p.id ? null : p.id); setConfirmId(null); }}
                title="Tap to change the date"
                style={{
                  border: "none", background: "transparent", padding: "0 0 2px", cursor: "pointer", textAlign: "left",
                  fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 15, color: "#1B1B1B",
                  borderBottom: editingId === p.id ? "2px solid var(--activity)" : "2px dotted #A9A192",
                }}
              >
                {p.date}
              </button>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, flex: "0 0 auto" }}>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12, color: "var(--activity)" }}>{p.duration} min</div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setConfirmId(confirmId === p.id ? null : p.id); setEditingId(null); }}
                  aria-label="Delete entry"
                  title="Delete entry"
                  style={{
                    width: 28, height: 28, borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #D8D0BF", background: "#F9F6EE",
                    display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", cursor: "pointer",
                    color: confirmId === p.id ? "#BE3B2A" : "#6E6759", flex: "0 0 auto", padding: 0,
                  }}
                >
                  <X size={13} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {editingId === p.id && (
              <div style={{ marginTop: 10, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                  {[0, 1, 2].map((daysBack) => {
                    const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() - daysBack);
                    const ds = localDateStr(d);
                    return (
                      <Chip key={daysBack} active={p.date === ds} onClick={() => onUpdateEntry(p.id, { date: ds })}>
                        {daysBack === 0 ? "Today" : daysBack === 1 ? "Yesterday" : "2 days ago"}
                      </Chip>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={p.date}
                  max={localDateStr()}
                  onChange={(ev) => ev.target.value && onUpdateEntry(p.id, { date: ev.target.value })}
                  style={{
                    width: "100%", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: "9px 12px",
                    fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 14, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
                  }}
                />
                <button onClick={() => setEditingId(null)} style={{ ...btnGhost, justifyContent: "center", padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>
                  Done
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {confirmId === p.id && (
              <div style={{ marginTop: 10, border: "2px solid #BE3B2A", background: "#F9E4DE", padding: "10px 12px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: 600, color: "#BE3B2A", lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                  Delete this entry? The journal text goes with it and there's no undo.
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                  <button onClick={() => setConfirmId(null)} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center", padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>
                    Keep it
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => { setConfirmId(null); onDeleteEntry(p.id); }} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "#BE3B2A", flex: 1, justifyContent: "center", padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>
                    Delete
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 8 }}>
              {practiceReadout(p)}
            </div>
            {p.journal && (
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 8, lineHeight: 1.6, fontStyle: "italic" }}>
                "{p.journal}"
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ))
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ---------- main app ----------
function App() {
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState(SEED_SESSIONS);
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState(DEFAULT_PLAN);
  const [practice, setPractice] = useState([]);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("today");
  const [logging, setLogging] = useState(false);
  const [practiceLogging, setPracticeLogging] = useState(false);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(null);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const [exercises, setExercises] = useState(DEFAULT_EXERCISES);
  const [stretches, setStretches] = useState(STRETCHES);
  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState(DEFAULT_RECIPES);
  const [mealPlan, setMealPlan] = useState([]);
  const [sila, setSila] = useState({});
  const [kitchenRules, setKitchenRules] = useState(SEED_RULES);
  const [saveWarning, setSaveWarning] = useState(null);
  const [showSettings, setShowSettings] = useState(false);
  const [teachings, setTeachings] = useState([]);
  const [apiKeyInput, setApiKeyInput] = useState("");
  const [hasKey, setHasKey] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setApiKeyInput(getApiKey());
    setHasKey(!!getApiKey());
    setDraft(loadDraft());
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadState().then(({ sessions: s, plan: p, practice: pr, exercises: ex, stretches: st, recipes: rc, mealPlan: mp, sila: sl, kitchenRules: kr, teachings: tg }) => {
      setSessions(s);
      if (p) setPlan(p);
      setPractice(pr || []);
      setExercises(ex || DEFAULT_EXERCISES);
      setStretches(st || STRETCHES);
      setRecipes(rc || DEFAULT_RECIPES);
      setMealPlan(mp || []);
      setSila(sl || {});
      setKitchenRules(Array.isArray(kr) ? kr : SEED_RULES);
      setTeachings(Array.isArray(tg) ? tg : []);
      setImportedTeachings(Array.isArray(tg) ? tg : []);
      setLoaded(true);
    });
  }, []);

  // The pool functions read a module-level mirror rather than a prop, so the
  // mirror has to be refreshed in the same breath as the state that owns it.
  const updateTeachings = useCallback((updater) => {
    setTeachings((prev) => {
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(prev) : updater;
      setImportedTeachings(next);
      saveTeachings(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);
  const updateExercises = useCallback((updater) => {
    setExercises((prev) => {
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(prev) : updater;
      saveExercises(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const updateRecipes = useCallback((updater) => {
    setRecipes((prev) => {
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(prev) : updater;
      saveRecipes(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const updateMealPlan = useCallback((updater) => {
    setMealPlan((prev) => {
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(prev) : updater;
      saveMealPlan(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  // One subscriber for every refused write in the app. The write layer names
  // the thing that didn't stick; this puts it on screen once, wherever he is.
  useEffect(() => onStorageFailure((what) => setSaveWarning(what)), []);

  const updateKitchenRules = useCallback((updater) => {
    setKitchenRules((prev) => {
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(prev) : updater;
      saveKitchenRules(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const updateSila = useCallback((updater) => {
    setSila((prev) => {
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(prev) : updater;
      saveSila(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const updateStretches = useCallback((updater) => {
    setStretches((prev) => {
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(prev) : updater;
      saveStretches(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const updatePractice = useCallback((updater) => {
    setPractice((prev) => {
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(prev) : updater;
      savePractice(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const handlePracticeComplete = useCallback((entry) => {
    updatePractice((prev) => [...prev, entry]);
    setPracticeLogging(false);
    setToast("Practice logged");
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2500);
  }, [updatePractice]);

  const handlePracticeUpdate = useCallback((id, patch) => {
    updatePractice((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, ...patch } : p)));
    setToast("Entry updated");
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2000);
  }, [updatePractice]);

  const handlePracticeDelete = useCallback((id) => {
    updatePractice((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id));
    setToast("Entry deleted");
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2000);
  }, [updatePractice]);

  const handleComplete = useCallback((entry) => {
    const updated = [...sessions, entry];
    setSessions(updated);
    saveSessions(updated);
    clearDraft();
    setDraft(null);
    setLogging(false);
    setTab("history");
    setToast("Session saved");
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2500);
  }, [sessions]);

  // Corrections to an already-saved session. The sort matters: a fixed date can
  // move a session out of insertion order, and everything that reads history —
  // the coach, the ladder, the A/B alternation — walks the array in order rather
  // than sorting first.
  const handleSessionUpdate = useCallback((id, patch) => {
    setSessions((prev) => {
      const next = prev.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...patch } : s));
      next.sort((a, b) => String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));
      saveSessions(next);
      return next;
    });
    setToast("Session updated");
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2000);
  }, []);

  const handleSessionDelete = useCallback((id) => {
    setSessions((prev) => {
      const next = prev.filter((s) => s.id !== id);
      saveSessions(next);
      return next;
    });
    setToast("Session deleted");
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2000);
  }, []);

  const lastSession = sessions[sessions.length - 1];
  const nextRestDays = 1;

  const runTrend = useMemo(() => sessions.filter(s => s.distance).map((s, i) => ({ i: i + 1, distance: s.distance, hr: s.hrAvg })), [sessions]);

  if (!loaded) return <div style={{ padding: 40, fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", color: "#6E6759" }}>Loading log…</div>;

  const TABS = [
    { key: "today", label: "Today" },
    { key: "practice", label: "Practice" },
    { key: "kitchen", label: "Kitchen" },
    { key: "history", label: "History" },
    { key: "plan", label: "Plan" },
  ];

  return (
    <div style={{
      minHeight: "100vh", backgroundColor: "#EFE9DC",
      backgroundImage: "radial-gradient(#DCD4C2 0.6px, transparent 0.7px)",
      backgroundSize: "4px 4px",
      fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif",
      padding: "28px 18px 60px",
    }}>
      <style>{`
        @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Archivo:wght@600;700;800;900&family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&family=IBM+Plex+Mono:wght@500;600&display=swap');
        * { box-sizing: border-box; }
        body { margin: 0; }
      `}</style>

      <div style={{ maxWidth: 520, margin: "0 auto" }}>
        <header style={{ marginBottom: 22, borderBottom: "3px solid #1B1B1B", paddingBottom: 10, display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-end" }}>
          <div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 42, fontWeight: 800, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 0.9, letterSpacing: -2, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
              Ledger
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, color: "#1B1B1B", letterSpacing: 1.4, textTransform: "uppercase", marginTop: 6 }}>
              <span style={{ background: "#FFC400", padding: "1px 5px" }}>No. {String(sessions.length).padStart(3, "0")}</span>
              <span style={{ color: "#6E6759", marginLeft: 8 }}>Flexible / Strong / Lean</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => setShowSettings(true)} style={{
            width: 34, height: 34, borderRadius: 0, border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", background: "#F9F6EE", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B",
            display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", cursor: "pointer", color: hasKey ? "var(--activity)" : "#BE3B2A",
          }} title={hasKey ? "API key set" : "Set API key for AI features"}>
            <Key size={16} />
          </button>
        </header>

        {showSettings && (
          <div style={{
            position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(32,35,42,0.5)", display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", zIndex: 50, padding: 20,
          }} onClick={() => setShowSettings(false)}>
            <div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", borderRadius: 0, padding: 24, maxWidth: 420, width: "100%" }}>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 19, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B", marginBottom: 6 }}>Anthropic API key</div>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.6, marginBottom: 14 }}>
                Needed for exercise how-to guides and the ask-a-question feature. Stored only in this browser's local storage — never sent anywhere except directly to Anthropic's API. Get a key at console.anthropic.com. Everything else in the app works fine without one.
              </div>
              <input
                type="password"
                value={apiKeyInput}
                onChange={(e) => setApiKeyInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="sk-ant-..."
                style={{ width: "100%", borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 12px", fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B" }}
              />
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 14 }}>
                <button onClick={() => setShowSettings(false)} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>Cancel</button>
                <button onClick={() => { setApiKey(apiKeyInput.trim()); setHasKey(!!apiKeyInput.trim()); setShowSettings(false); }} style={{ ...btnPrimary, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>Save</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {!logging && (
          <nav style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${TABS.length}, 1fr)`, gap: 0, marginBottom: 22, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B" }}>
          {TABS.map((t, i) => (
            <button key={t.key} onClick={() => setTab(t.key)} style={{
              padding: "11px 2px", border: "none", cursor: "pointer",
              borderLeft: i === 0 ? "none" : "2px solid #1B1B1B",
              background: tab === t.key ? "#1B1B1B" : "#F9F6EE",
              color: tab === t.key ? "#F9F6EE" : "#1B1B1B",
              fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5,
              fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: 0.8, textTransform: "uppercase",
            }}>{t.label}</button>
          ))}
        </nav>
        )}

        {saveWarning && (
          <div style={{ border: "3px solid #BE3B2A", background: "#F9E4DE", padding: 14, marginBottom: 22 }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: 1.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#BE3B2A", marginBottom: 6 }}>
              Not saved
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
              {saveWarning.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + saveWarning.slice(1)} is showing here but did not write to storage, so it will not survive a restart. Your phone's storage for this app is probably full — export from the Plan tab, then tell me.
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => setSaveWarning(null)} style={{ ...btnGhost, marginTop: 10, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 12 }}>Dismiss</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {!logging && !practiceLogging && draft && (
          <div style={{ border: "3px solid #1B1B1B", background: "#FFC400", padding: 14, marginBottom: 22 }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: 1.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B", marginBottom: 6 }}>
              Unfinished workout
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.55, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              {draftSummary(draft)}
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <button onClick={() => setLogging(true)} style={{ ...btnPrimary, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>Resume</button>
              <button onClick={() => { clearDraft(); setDraft(null); }} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: "0 0 auto", justifyContent: "center" }}>Discard</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {logging ? (
          <LogWizard sessions={sessions} exercises={exercises} stretches={stretches} initialDraft={draft} onCancel={() => { clearDraft(); setDraft(null); setLogging(false); }} onComplete={handleComplete} />
        ) : practiceLogging ? (
          <PracticeWizard practice={practice} onCancel={() => setPracticeLogging(false)} onComplete={handlePracticeComplete} />
        ) : tab === "today" ? (
          <TodayTab sessions={sessions} plan={plan} exercises={exercises} setExercises={updateExercises} stretches={stretches} setStretches={updateStretches} onStartLog={() => { clearDraft(); setDraft(null); setLogging(true); }} sila={sila} setSila={updateSila} />
        ) : tab === "practice" ? (
          <PracticeTab
            practice={practice}
            onStartLog={() => setPracticeLogging(true)}
            onUpdateEntry={handlePracticeUpdate}
            onDeleteEntry={handlePracticeDelete}
            teachings={teachings}
            setTeachings={updateTeachings}
          />
        ) : tab === "kitchen" ? (
          <KitchenTab recipes={recipes} setRecipes={updateRecipes} mealPlan={mealPlan} setMealPlan={updateMealPlan} sessions={sessions} rules={kitchenRules} setRules={updateKitchenRules} />
        ) : tab === "history" ? (
          <HistoryTab sessions={sessions} exercises={exercises} allStretches={stretches} recipes={recipes} mealPlan={mealPlan} onUpdateSession={handleSessionUpdate} onDeleteSession={handleSessionDelete} />
        ) : (
          <PlanTab plan={plan} setPlan={(p) => { setPlan(p); savePlan(p); }} sessions={sessions} exercises={exercises} setExercises={updateExercises} stretches={stretches} setStretches={updateStretches} recipes={recipes} mealPlan={mealPlan} practice={practice} sila={sila} kitchenRules={kitchenRules} teachings={teachings} />
        )}

        {toast && (
          <div style={{
            position: "fixed", bottom: 24, left: "50%", transform: "translateX(-50%)",
            background: "#1B1B1B", color: "#F9F6EE", padding: "10px 20px", borderRadius: 0,
            fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500,
            display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, boxShadow: "5px 5px 0 #75695A",
          }}>
            <Check size={15} /> {toast}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// During the period, the day's precept sits directly above the journal field —
// close enough that answering it is the path of least resistance, and worded as
// a question about the day rather than a box to tick.
function SilaJournalPrompt() {
  const today = localDateStr();
  const period = silaPeriod(today);
  if (!period) return null;
  const n = preceptOfDay(today);
  const precept = PRECEPTS.find((x) => x.n === n);
  if (!precept) return null;
  // Under the direct-teaching rule this closing line is a teacher's own words
  // whenever the library holds one for the precept in view. The written
  // question is the floor, not the default.
  const passage = preceptPassage(today, n);
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", background: "#F9F6EE", padding: "10px 12px", marginBottom: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 6 }}>
        Sila period · Day {period.dayNumber} of {period.totalDays}
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
        {precept.text}
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.6, marginTop: 6 }}>
        {passage ? passage.text : "Where was this easy today, and where did it ask something of you?"}
      </div>
      {passage && passage.source && (
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 5 }}>{passage.source}</div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// The band on Today. It names the period, holds the five precepts in view with
// the day's one picked out, and carries the two markers.
function SilaBand({ sila, setSila }) {
  const today = localDateStr();
  const period = silaPeriod(today);
  if (!period) return null;

  const record = (sila && sila[period.year]) || {};
  const opened = record.opened || null;
  const closed = record.closed || null;
  const activeN = preceptOfDay(today);
  const pointer = dailyPointerFor(today, null, 0);
  const isSilaPointer = !!(pointer && pointer.precept !== undefined);
  const activePrecept = PRECEPTS.find((x) => x.n === activeN);

  const mark = (field) => setSila((prev) => {
    const base = prev || {};
    return { ...base, [period.year]: { ...(base[period.year] || {}), [field]: today } };
  });

  const MONTHS = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
  const stamp = (ds) => {
    if (!ds || ds.length < 10) return "";
    const m = MONTHS[Number(ds.slice(5, 7)) - 1];
    return m ? `${m} ${Number(ds.slice(8, 10))}` : ds;
  };

  const headline = !opened
    ? (period.isOpeningDay ? "Opens today" : "Not yet undertaken")
    : closed ? "Closed"
    : period.isClosingDay ? "Closes today"
    : `Day ${period.dayNumber}`;

  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: 20 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline", gap: 10 }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: 1.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>
          Sila period
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, color: "#6E6759" }}>
          Day {period.dayNumber} / {period.totalDays}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.9, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B", margin: "4px 0 14px" }}>
        {headline}
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B" }}>
        {PRECEPTS.map((pr, i) => {
          const on = pr.n === activeN;
          return (
            <div key={pr.n} style={{
              display: "flex", gap: 10, alignItems: "flex-start",
              padding: "8px 10px",
              borderTop: i === 0 ? "none" : "1px solid #D8D0BF",
              background: on ? "#1B1B1B" : "#F9F6EE",
              color: on ? "#F9F6EE" : "#1B1B1B",
            }}>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, opacity: on ? 0.7 : 0.55, flex: "0 0 auto", paddingTop: 1 }}>
                {pr.n}
              </div>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, lineHeight: 1.5, fontWeight: on ? 600 : 400 }}>
                {pr.text}
                {on && (
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 400, opacity: 0.75, marginTop: 4, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
                    {pr.gloss}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {isSilaPointer && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 5 }}>
            Today
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13.5, lineHeight: 1.6, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
            {pointer.text}
          </div>
          {pointer.source && (
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 6 }}>
              {pointer.source}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {!opened ? (
        <button onClick={() => mark("opened")} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "var(--activity)", color: "#F9F6EE", marginTop: 16 }}>
          Undertake the period
        </button>
      ) : !closed && period.isClosingDay ? (
        <>
          <button onClick={() => mark("closed")} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "var(--activity)", color: "#F9F6EE", marginTop: 16 }}>
            Close the period
          </button>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 8 }}>
            Undertaken {stamp(opened)}
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 14 }}>
          Undertaken {stamp(opened)}{closed ? ` · closed ${stamp(closed)}` : ""}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function TodayTab({ sessions, plan, exercises, setExercises, stretches = STRETCHES, setStretches, onStartLog, sila, setSila }) {
  const todayStr = localDateStr();
  const todaysSession = sessions.find((s) => s.date === todayStr);
  const nextType = useMemo(() => {
    const last = sessions[sessions.length - 1];
    if (!last) return "A";
    // Alternate A/B; light/outdoor days don't break the cycle
    const lastReal = [...sessions].reverse().find(s => effectiveType(s, exercises) === "A" || effectiveType(s, exercises) === "B");
    return effectiveType(lastReal || {}, exercises) === "A" ? "B" : "A";
  }, [sessions, exercises]);

  const [showExpansionDetail, setShowExpansionDetail] = useState(false);
  const coach = useMemo(() => computeCoachSummary(sessions, exercises, plan, nextType, stretches), [sessions, exercises, plan, nextType, stretches]);
  const sessionMinutes = useMemo(
    () => estimateSessionMinutes(exercises, stretches, nextType, coach.cardio.duration, sessions),
    [exercises, stretches, nextType, coach.cardio.duration, sessions]
  );
  const expansion = useMemo(
    () => suggestExerciseExpansion(sessions, exercises, stretches, nextType, plan.sessionBudgetMinutes || 120, coach.cardio.duration),
    [sessions, exercises, stretches, nextType, plan.sessionBudgetMinutes, coach.cardio.duration]
  );
  const stretchExpansion = useMemo(
    () => suggestStretchExpansion(sessions, stretches),
    [sessions, stretches]
  );

  // Pull a movement out of the library and into the active routine, carrying
  // its pre-written explainer so it works with no API key.
  const addFromLibrary = (item) => {
    setExercises((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [item.key]: {
        label: item.label, unit: item.unit, targetSets: item.targetSets, targetReps: item.targetReps,
        step: item.step, noWeight: item.noWeight, sessionType: item.sessionType,
        patterns: item.patterns, explainer: item.explainer, estMinutes: item.estMinutes,
        active: true, addedAt: localDateStr(),
      },
    }));
    setShowExpansionDetail(false);
  };

  const addStretchFromLibrary = (item) => {
    setStretches((prev) => (prev.some((s) => s.key === item.key) ? prev : [...prev, { ...item, active: true }]));
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>
      <div style={{
        background: "#1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: 24, color: "#F9F6EE", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #75695A",
      }}>
        {todaysSession ? (
          <>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.6, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>Today · logged</div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 38, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -1.4, textTransform: "uppercase", margin: "4px 0 14px" }}>Session {["A", "B"].includes(effectiveType(todaysSession, exercises)) ? effectiveType(todaysSession, exercises) : "done"}</div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, opacity: 0.75, lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
              {todaysSession.distance ? `${todaysSession.distance}mi` : ""}{todaysSession.hrAvg ? ` · HR ${todaysSession.hrAvg}/${todaysSession.hrMax}` : ""}{todaysSession.sauna ? " · sauna" : ""}
            </div>
            <button onClick={onStartLog} style={{ ...btnGhost, borderColor: "#F9F6EE", color: "#F9F6EE", marginTop: 18 }}>
              Log another entry for today
            </button>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.6, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>Up next</div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 38, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -1.4, textTransform: "uppercase", margin: "4px 0 14px" }}>Session {nextType}</div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, opacity: 0.75, lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
              {["Treadmill", "Movement prep", ...coach.directives.map((d) => d.label), "Stretches", "Sauna"].join(" · ")}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.55, marginTop: 8 }}>
              ~{sessionMinutes} min estimated
              {coach.rest.daysSinceLast != null && (
                coach.rest.daysSinceLast === 0 ? " · last session today" : ` · last session ${coach.rest.daysSinceLast}d ago`
              )}
            </div>
            <button onClick={onStartLog} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 18 }}>
              Log today's session <ChevronRight size={16} />
            </button>
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <SilaBand sila={sila} setSila={setSila} />

      {(coach.rest.flag || coach.stretchFreq.flag || coach.balance.flag) && (
        <div style={{ background: "#E5EDE4", border: "2px solid #2F6B4F", borderRadius: 0, padding: 20 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#2F6B4F", marginBottom: 6 }}>Recovery &amp; mobility</div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
            {coach.rest.flag && (
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>{coach.rest.flag}</div>
            )}
            {coach.stretchFreq.flag && (
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>{coach.stretchFreq.flag}</div>
            )}
            {coach.balance.flag && (
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>{coach.balance.flag}</div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {!todaysSession && (
        <div style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: 20 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B", marginBottom: 4 }}>Coach's plan for today</div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 12, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
            Cardio: {coach.cardio.distance}mi / {coach.cardio.duration} min — {coach.cardio.rationale}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
            {coach.directives.map((d) => (
              <div key={d.key} style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 10 }}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
                  <span>{d.label}</span>
                  <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12.5, color: "var(--activity)" }}>
                    {d.rec?.weight != null ? `${d.rec.weight}${exercises[d.key].unit} · ` : ""}{d.rec?.sets ? `${d.rec.sets}×` : ""}{d.rec?.reps}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 2, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                  {d.rec?.rationale}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", marginTop: 12, paddingTop: 10 }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B", marginBottom: 6 }}>
              Stretches after
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
              {coach.stretches.map((s) => (
                <div key={s.key} style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759" }}>
                  <span>{s.label}</span>
                  <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", color: "var(--activity)" }}>
                    {s.cfg.holdSeconds}s{s.cfg.perSide ? "/side" : ""} × {s.rec.rounds}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {coach.plateaus.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ background: "#E5EDE4", border: "2px solid #2F6B4F", borderRadius: 0, padding: 20 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#2F6B4F", marginBottom: 6 }}>Needs attention</div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
            {coach.plateaus.map((p, i) => (
              <div key={i} style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>{p}</div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {expansion.ready && (
        <div style={{ background: "var(--activity-tint)", border: "2px solid var(--activity)", borderRadius: 0, padding: 20 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "var(--activity)" }}>
            Ready to add
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.7, color: "#1B1B1B", margin: "4px 0 2px" }}>
            {expansion.suggestion.label}
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "var(--activity)", marginBottom: 8 }}>
            Fills a gap: {expansion.gapLabel} · Session {expansion.suggestion.sessionType}
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            {expansion.reason}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 14 }}>
            <button onClick={() => addFromLibrary(expansion.suggestion)} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "var(--activity)", color: "#F9F6EE", padding: "10px 16px", fontSize: 13 }}>
              Add to routine
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setShowExpansionDetail((v) => !v)} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "10px 16px", fontSize: 13 }}>
              {showExpansionDetail ? "Hide how-to" : "How to do it"}
            </button>
          </div>
          {showExpansionDetail && (
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.7, background: "#F9F6EE", borderRadius: 0, padding: 14, marginTop: 10, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }}>
              {expansion.suggestion.explainer}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {!expansion.ready && expansion.reason && (
        <div style={{ background: expansion.atCapacity ? "#E5EDE4" : "#F9F6EE", border: `1.5px solid ${expansion.atCapacity ? "#2F6B4F" : "#D8D0BF"}`, borderRadius: 0, padding: 18 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: expansion.atCapacity ? "#2F6B4F" : "#1B1B1B", marginBottom: 4 }}>
            {expansion.atCapacity ? "Session at time capacity" : "Next movement to add"}
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
            {expansion.reason}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {stretchExpansion.ready && (
        <div style={{ background: "var(--activity-tint)", border: "2px solid var(--activity)", borderRadius: 0, padding: 20 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "var(--activity)" }}>
            Ready to add — stretch
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.7, color: "#1B1B1B", margin: "4px 0 2px" }}>
            {stretchExpansion.suggestion.label}
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "var(--activity)", marginBottom: 8 }}>
            {stretchExpansion.suggestion.muscle} · {stretchExpansion.suggestion.holdSeconds}s
            {stretchExpansion.suggestion.perSide ? "/side" : ""}
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            {stretchExpansion.reason}
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => addStretchFromLibrary(stretchExpansion.suggestion)} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "var(--activity)", color: "#F9F6EE", padding: "10px 16px", fontSize: 13, marginTop: 14 }}>
            Add to routine
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 4 }}>Progression</div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
        <RunGraph sessions={sessions} />
        {Object.keys(exercises).filter(k => {
          const cfg = exercises[k];
          if (cfg.active === false) return false;
          if (!cfg.noWeight) return true;
          return sessions.some(s => s.exercises && s.exercises[k] && s.exercises[k].weight);
        }).map((k) => (
          <ProgressionLadder key={k} sessions={sessions} exKey={k} exercises={exercises} />
        ))}
      </div>

      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 4 }}>Queued</div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
        {plan.queue.filter(q => !sessions.some(s => s.exercises && s.exercises[q.key])).map((q, i) => (
          <div key={i} style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{exercises[q.key]?.label || q.key}</div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 2 }}>{q.note}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



// ---------- chef ----------
// The chef prompt carries every hard-won constraint from months of actual
// cooking, so a generated recipe arrives already knowing the oven runs hot,
// which greens survive five days, and how the food gets stored. The verdicts
