// recorded on recipe cards get appended to it, so a batch that failed once
// becomes a rule the chef obeys forever instead of a lesson only I remember.
const CHEF_OPENING = `You are a Michelin-level chef whose specialty is healthy food that a single person actually wants to eat. Your food is genuinely delicious and technique-driven, never bland "meal prep" food, but the methods must stay achievable on a weeknight.`;

const CHEF_TAIL = `NUTRITION TARGET: this person lifts three times a week and wants 25-40g of protein per serving. Hit that range. Estimate protein and fiber per serving honestly.

Every recipe must include a TECHNIQUE note: the one piece of craft that makes this dish better than the obvious version of itself, explained in a way that teaches something.

TWO SEPARATE LISTS ARE REQUIRED, and they are not the same list:

1. "ingredients" — what the recipe uses, in cooking quantities, in the order they are used. Every single thing the method touches appears here, including salt, oil and water. Write them the way a recipe writes them: "2 lbs boneless chicken thighs", "1.5 tsp ground cumin", "3 tbsp extra virgin olive oil". Do not write package sizes here.

2. "grocery" — what to put in the cart, sorted into supermarket departments, named EXACTLY as the shelf tag or sign reads at Big Y or Market Basket so it can be found without hunting. Give exact package and can sizes and the number to buy: "3 x 15.5 oz cans Goya chickpeas", "1 x 5 oz bag baby arugula", "2 lbs boneless skinless chicken thighs (family pack)". Say the department name the store actually uses. Leave a department out entirely if nothing is bought there — do not include empty arrays with blank strings. Omit staples that any kitchen already has ONLY if they are truly universal (salt, black pepper, olive oil); if a spice is at all specific, buy it. Use "notes" for anything worth knowing at the store: which chain is likelier to carry something, a decent substitute if it is missing, or where in the store an odd item hides.`;

const SCOPE_PREP = `SCOPE: a week of make-ahead lunches. Five servings per batch, stored BY COMPONENT in bins (not portioned containers). Sauces always stored separately and added at lunch. Everything must still be good on day five. Set "scope" to "prep" and "servings" to 5.`;

const SCOPE_MEAL = `SCOPE: one meal, cooked and eaten the same day. This is NOT meal prep — do not optimise for five-day storage, do not store by component, and feel free to use delicate ingredients that would never survive a week. Cook one generous serving. Keep it under 45 minutes. Set "scope" to "meal" and "servings" to 1.`;

const ANGLES = {
  onhand: `ANGLE: use up what is already in the kitchen. Treat the listed ingredients as the starting point and build around them. Keep the shopping list as short as possible — only what genuinely must be bought.`,
  new: `ANGLE: teach something new. Deliberately reach for a technique, cuisine or ingredient this kitchen has NOT cooked before (see the history below). The point is to widen the repertoire, not to play it safe. Say plainly in the technique note what is new here and why it is worth learning.`,
  fast: `ANGLE: minimum effort. Shortcuts are welcome — rotisserie chicken, pre-cut vegetables, jarred staples. Keep active time low and say the honest number. It should still taste like someone cooked it.`,
  proven: `ANGLE: something reliable. Lean on techniques and flavour profiles that have already worked in this kitchen (see the history below). Comfort over novelty this time.`,
};

const CHEF_JSON = `Respond with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fence, no preamble. Omit any grocery department where nothing is bought:
{"name":"","scope":"prep","servings":5,"cuisine":"","protein":"","grain":"","veg":"","sauce":"","proteinG":0,"fiberG":0,"activeMinutes":0,"prepNotes":"","technique":"","ingredients":["2 lbs boneless chicken thighs","1.5 tsp ground cumin"],"method":[{"t":"step title","d":"step detail"}],"grocery":{"produce":["1 x 5 oz bag baby arugula"],"meatSeafood":[],"deli":[],"dairy":[],"bakery":[],"cannedJarred":[],"dryGoods":[],"spices":[],"frozen":[],"other":[],"notes":""}}`;

// A verdict is only worth recording if it changes what gets cooked next, so
// every rejection and every win is folded back into the prompt as a rule.
function kitchenHistory(recipes) {
  const list = recipes || [];
  const rejected = list.filter((r) => r.verdict === "never");
  const loved = list.filter((r) => r.verdict === "loved");
  const cooked = list.filter((r) => r.lastMade);
  const blocks = [];

  if (rejected.length) {
    blocks.push(
      "REJECTED IN THIS KITCHEN — these were actually cooked and did not work. Do not repeat the underlying mistake, and do not propose a near-variant:\n" +
      rejected.map((r) => `- ${r.name}${r.verdictNote ? ` — what went wrong: ${r.verdictNote}` : ""}`).join("\n")
    );
  }
  if (loved.length) {
    blocks.push(
      "WORKED WELL — safe reference points for what this palate enjoys:\n" +
      loved.map((r) => `- ${r.name}${r.cuisine ? ` (${r.cuisine})` : ""}`).join("\n")
    );
  }
  const adjusted = list.filter((r) => Array.isArray(r.edits) && r.edits.length);
  if (adjusted.length) {
    blocks.push(
      "ADJUSTMENTS MADE AT THE STOVE — these are the cook's own corrections to recipes already made here. Treat them as evidence about this palate and this kitchen, and carry the lesson into anything similar:\n" +
      adjusted.map((r) => `- ${r.name}:\n` + r.edits.map((e) => `    · ${e.text}`).join("\n")).join("\n")
    );
  }
  if (cooked.length) {
    const names = cooked.map((r) => r.name).join("; ");
    const cuisines = Array.from(new Set(cooked.map((r) => r.cuisine).filter(Boolean)));
    blocks.push(
      `ALREADY COOKED — do not propose these again or a thin variation of them: ${names}.` +
      (cuisines.length ? `\nCuisines already covered: ${cuisines.join(", ")}. Prefer territory outside this list.` : "")
    );
  }
  return blocks.length ? "\n\n" + blocks.join("\n\n") : "";
}

function buildChefSystem(recipes, scope, angle, rules) {
  return [
    CHEF_OPENING,
    rulesBlock(rules),
    CHEF_TAIL,
    scope === "meal" ? SCOPE_MEAL : SCOPE_PREP,
    ANGLES[angle] || "",
    kitchenHistory(recipes),
    CHEF_JSON,
  ].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n");
}

// Bringing in someone else's recipe. The chef is a converter here, not an
// author: it fits the dish to this kitchen and says what it changed, and the
// cook whose recipe it is stays named on the card.
const CHEF_IMPORT = `SCOPE: convert a recipe that came from a named cook into this kitchen's format.

You did not write this recipe and you are not replacing its author. Your job is to fit it to this kitchen and to be honest about every change you made.

- Keep the dish recognisably the same dish. Do not redesign it, do not rename it into something else, and do not quietly substitute the thing that makes it what it is.
- Apply the constraints above where they genuinely conflict with the original, and nowhere else. Leaving the recipe alone is a valid outcome.
- Scale it to the servings asked for and recalculate quantities honestly.
- Put every change you made into "adaptations" as short plain lines: what you changed and why. If you changed nothing, return an empty array.
- The "technique" note should teach the craft the original author was actually using, not a technique you prefer.
- Estimate protein and fiber per serving honestly. If the dish simply does not reach the protein target, say so in "adaptations" rather than padding it with an ingredient the author never used.`;

const CHEF_IMPORT_JSON = `Respond with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fence, no preamble. Same shape as a generated recipe plus "adaptations". Omit any grocery department where nothing is bought:
{"name":"","scope":"meal","servings":1,"cuisine":"","protein":"","grain":"","veg":"","sauce":"","proteinG":0,"fiberG":0,"activeMinutes":0,"prepNotes":"","technique":"","adaptations":["halved the butter"],"ingredients":["2 lbs boneless chicken thighs"],"method":[{"t":"step title","d":"step detail"}],"grocery":{"produce":[],"meatSeafood":[],"deli":[],"dairy":[],"bakery":[],"cannedJarred":[],"dryGoods":[],"spices":[],"frozen":[],"other":[],"notes":""}}`;

// Reading the page rather than being handed a clean recipe: the author's name,
// the publication, and the upstream source all have to come off the page, and
// the comment thread is worth mining because it is the one thing a generated
// recipe has no equivalent of — dozens of people reporting what actually
// happened when they cooked it.
const CHEF_URL_IMPORT = `You are reading a recipe page on the web and bringing the recipe into this kitchen.

FIRST, from the page itself, identify:
- "cook": the person who wrote this recipe. Their actual name, as the page gives it. If the page only shows a handle or a site name, use that. Never guess a name that is not on the page — leave it empty instead.
- "site": the name of the publication or blog it appears on.
- "upstream": if the author says they adapted it from someone else — another cook, a cookbook — record that as "Name, Source". Empty if they don't say.
- "commentNotes": if the page has a comment thread, read it. Pull out only what is repeated by several independent people or confirmed by the author, and only where it would change how the dish is cooked — a consistent complaint that it comes out too salty, a substitution many people report working, a step that catches people out. Two or three short lines at most. Empty array if the comments say nothing useful or there are none.

THEN convert the recipe for this kitchen:
- Keep the dish recognisably the same dish. Do not redesign it, do not rename it, and do not substitute the thing that makes it what it is.
- Apply the kitchen's constraints above only where they genuinely conflict with the original. Leaving the recipe alone is a valid outcome.
- Scale to the servings asked for and recalculate quantities honestly.
- Put every change you made into "adaptations" as short plain lines. Empty array if you changed nothing.
- If what the comments report contradicts the recipe as written, follow the recipe but say so in "adaptations" or "commentNotes" — don't silently overrule the author.
- The "technique" note should teach the craft the original author was using, not one you prefer.
- Estimate protein and fiber per serving honestly. If the dish does not reach the protein target, say so in "adaptations" rather than padding it with an ingredient the author never used.

If the page turns out not to contain a recipe, return {"error":"no recipe on that page"} and nothing else.`;

const CHEF_URL_JSON = `Respond with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fence, no preamble. Omit any grocery department where nothing is bought:
{"name":"","cook":"","site":"","upstream":"","commentNotes":["several people found it too salty with regular soy sauce"],"scope":"meal","servings":1,"cuisine":"","protein":"","grain":"","veg":"","sauce":"","proteinG":0,"fiberG":0,"activeMinutes":0,"prepNotes":"","technique":"","adaptations":["halved the butter"],"ingredients":["2 lbs boneless chicken thighs"],"method":[{"t":"step title","d":"step detail"}],"grocery":{"produce":[],"meatSeafood":[],"deli":[],"dairy":[],"bakery":[],"cannedJarred":[],"dryGoods":[],"spices":[],"frozen":[],"other":[],"notes":""}}`;

function buildUrlImportSystem(recipes, rules) {
  return [
    CHEF_OPENING,
    rulesBlock(rules),
    CHEF_URL_IMPORT,
    kitchenHistory(recipes),
    CHEF_URL_JSON,
  ].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n");
}

function buildImportSystem(recipes, rules) {
  return [
    CHEF_OPENING,
    rulesBlock(rules),
    CHEF_IMPORT,
    kitchenHistory(recipes),
    CHEF_IMPORT_JSON,
  ].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n");
}

// The recipe page is fetched by the API itself rather than by the app. A
// browser can't fetch smittenkitchen.com from a page served off github.io —
// CORS forbids it — and a proxy would be one more thing to break. The web
// fetch tool runs server-side during the request, and it will only touch a URL
// that already appeared in the conversation, which here means the one pasted
// into the box.
async function askChefUrl(request, system, url) {
  const key = getApiKey();
  if (!key) throw new Error("no-key");

  const call = (messages) => fetch("https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "x-api-key": key,
      "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
      "anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access": "true",
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      model: "claude-sonnet-4-6",
      // An imported recipe runs longer than a generated one — it carries the
      // adaptations, what the comments turned up, and the chef's own narration
      // of the fetch, all of which count as output. At 3000 this ran out
      // mid-array and the JSON came back unclosed.
      max_tokens: 8000,
      system,
      tools: [{
        type: "web_fetch_20250910",
        name: "web_fetch",
        max_uses: 3,
        // Recipe blogs run long — comment threads on a popular post can be
        // hundreds of entries. The recipe sits well above them, so truncation
        // costs nothing and keeps a single import from burning tokens.
        max_content_tokens: 30000,
      }],
      messages,
    }),
  });

  let messages = [{ role: "user", content: request }];
  let blocks = [];
  let stop = null;

  // A server-side fetch can take long enough that the API returns early with
  // stop_reason "pause_turn", expecting the conversation handed straight back
  // so it can carry on. Without this the reply arrives half-finished and looks
  // exactly like a malformed recipe.
  for (let turn = 0; turn < 3; turn++) {
    const response = await call(messages);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`API error ${response.status}`);
    const data = await response.json();
    blocks = blocks.concat(data.content || []);
    stop = data.stop_reason;
    if (stop === "pause_turn") {
      messages = messages.concat([{ role: "assistant", content: data.content }]);
      continue;
    }
    break;
  }

  // A failed fetch comes back as a 200 with the error inside the body, so the
  // only way to tell the cook something useful is to go looking for it.
  const fetchError = blocks
    .filter((c) => c.type === "web_fetch_tool_result")
    .map((c) => c.content && c.content.error_code)
    .find(Boolean);
  if (fetchError) throw new Error("fetch:" + fetchError);

  const text = blocks.filter((c) => c.type === "text").map((c) => c.text).join("");
  const cleaned = text.replace(/```json/g, "").replace(/```/g, "").trim();
  const first = cleaned.indexOf("{");
  const last = cleaned.lastIndexOf("}");
  if (first < 0 || last < 0) {
    if (stop === "max_tokens") throw new Error("truncated");
    throw new Error("Could not read a recipe from that page.");
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(cleaned.slice(first, last + 1));
  } catch (e) {
    // Unclosed JSON almost always means the reply was cut off rather than that
    // the chef wrote something invalid, so say the useful thing.
    throw new Error(stop === "max_tokens" ? "truncated" : "unreadable");
  }
}

const CHEF_ERRORS = {
  truncated: "That recipe came back longer than the chef had room for. Try it with fewer servings, or paste the recipe text in instead.",
  unreadable: "The chef's reply came back garbled. Worth trying once more.",
};

const FETCH_ERRORS = {
  url_not_accessible: "That page wouldn't load — it may be down, or behind a login.",
  url_not_allowed: "That site doesn't allow automated fetching.",
  unsupported_content_type: "That link isn't a web page or PDF.",
  url_too_long: "That link is too long to fetch.",
  too_many_requests: "Rate limited — wait a minute and try again.",
  invalid_tool_input: "That doesn't look like a valid link.",
};

async function askChef(request, system) {
  const key = getApiKey();
  if (!key) throw new Error("no-key");
  const response = await fetch("https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "x-api-key": key,
      "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
      "anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access": "true",
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      model: "claude-sonnet-4-6",
      // A five-serving prep with a full grocery list gets close to the old
      // 2000 ceiling; the imported path hit exactly that wall.
      max_tokens: 6000,
      system,
      messages: [{ role: "user", content: request }],
    }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`API error ${response.status}`);
  const data = await response.json();
  const text = (data.content || []).filter((c) => c.type === "text").map((c) => c.text).join("");
  const cleaned = text.replace(/```json/g, "").replace(/```/g, "").trim();
  const first = cleaned.indexOf("{");
  const last = cleaned.lastIndexOf("}");
  if (first < 0 || last < 0) {
    throw new Error(data.stop_reason === "max_tokens" ? "truncated" : "Could not read a recipe from the response.");
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(cleaned.slice(first, last + 1));
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error(data.stop_reason === "max_tokens" ? "truncated" : "unreadable");
  }
}

const importField = {
  width: "100%", padding: "9px 10px", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0,
  fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
};

const ANGLE_LABELS = [
  { key: "onhand", label: "Use what I have", hint: "type what's in the fridge below" },
  { key: "new", label: "Teach me something new", hint: "pushes into untried territory" },
  { key: "fast", label: "Fast & easy", hint: "shortcuts welcome" },
  { key: "proven", label: "Something reliable", hint: "leans on what has worked" },
];

// A generated recipe costs an API call and several minutes of reading, and it
// used to live only in component state — so backgrounding the app on a phone,
// which Android will happily do, threw it away. It now hits storage the moment
// it arrives and is only cleared once it has been saved or explicitly discarded.
const CHEF_DRAFT_KEY = "chef-draft";

function loadChefDraft() {
  try {
    const raw = lsGet(CHEF_DRAFT_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw.value);
    return parsed && parsed.draft ? parsed : null;
  } catch (e) { return null; }
}

// Selecting text in a textarea on Android is fiddly enough that the list was
// effectively read-only. A real copy button is the whole point of the feature.
function GroceryList({ recipe }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const text = buildGroceryList(recipe);

  const copy = async () => {
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      } else {
        const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
        ta.value = text;
        ta.style.position = "fixed";
        ta.style.opacity = "0";
        document.body.appendChild(ta);
        ta.select();
        document.execCommand("copy");
        document.body.removeChild(ta);
      }
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (e) {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, marginBottom: 6 }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", flex: 1 }}>
          Big Y · Market Basket · Food City
        </div>
        <button onClick={copy} style={{
          ...btnPrimary, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 12,
          background: copied ? "var(--activity)" : "#1B1B1B",
        }}>
          {copied ? "Copied" : "Copy list"}
        </button>
      </div>
      <textarea
        readOnly
        onClick={(e) => e.target.select()}
        value={text}
        style={{
          width: "100%", minHeight: 260, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 12,
          fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 1.6,
          background: "#E7E0D0", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

// The cook's own knowledge base. Freewriting is the entry point on purpose:
// composing an instruction is a different, harder task than saying what
// happened, and asking for the harder one at the stove means it never gets
// written down at all. The chef does the conversion and hands it back to edit.
function KitchenRules({ rules, setRules }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(null);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null);
  const list = rules || [];

  const distill = async () => {
    const body = note.trim();
    if (!body) { setError("Write what happened first — a sentence or two is plenty."); return; }
    setBusy(true); setError(null);
    try {
      const result = await askChef(body, RULE_DISTILL_SYSTEM);
      setPending({ text: (result.text || "").trim() || body, why: (result.why || "").trim() });
    } catch (e) {
      // No key, no signal, or a refusal to parse — none of which should cost
      // the cook the thing they just wrote. Keep it verbatim and let them edit.
      setPending({ text: body, why: "" });
      setError(e.message === "no-key"
        ? "Saved as written — sharpening it into a rule needs your API key, but the note is kept either way."
        : "Saved as written — could not reach the chef to sharpen it. Edit it by hand if you like.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  const commit = () => {
    if (!pending || !pending.text.trim()) return;
    const rule = { id: "r" + uid(), text: pending.text.trim(), why: (pending.why || "").trim(), added: localDateStr() };
    setRules((prev) => [...(prev || []), rule]);
    setPending(null); setNote(""); setError(null);
  };

  const update = (id, field, value) => {
    setRules((prev) => (prev || []).map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, [field]: value } : r)));
  };
  const remove = (id) => {
    setRules((prev) => (prev || []).filter((r) => r.id !== id));
    setEditingId(null);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", background: "#F9F6EE", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B" }}>
      <button onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} style={{
        width: "100%", textAlign: "left", background: "none", border: "none", padding: 16, cursor: "pointer", display: "block",
      }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
          What this kitchen knows
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 4, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
          {list.length} rule{list.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} the chef obeys · tap to {open ? "close" : "add or prune"}
        </div>
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div style={{ borderTop: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 16, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 14 }}>

          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            Write what happened at the stove — rough is fine, don't compose it. It gets turned into an instruction the chef follows, and you get the last word before it's saved.
          </div>

          <textarea
            value={note}
            onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="the soy sauce was way too salty even the low sodium one, next time cut it with water. also the eggplant skin went leathery, maybe the oven was too hot"
            style={{
              width: "100%", minHeight: 88, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 10,
              fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
            }}
          />

          {!pending && (
            <button onClick={distill} disabled={busy} style={{
              ...btnPrimary, opacity: busy ? 0.6 : 1, padding: "10px 14px", fontSize: 12.5, alignSelf: "flex-start",
            }}>
              {busy ? "Working…" : "Make this a rule"}
            </button>
          )}

          {error && (
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {pending && (
            <div style={{ border: "2px solid var(--activity)", background: "var(--activity-tint)", padding: 14, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "var(--activity)" }}>
                Yours to edit before it saves
              </div>
              <textarea
                value={pending.text}
                onChange={(e) => setPending({ ...pending, text: e.target.value })}
                style={{
                  width: "100%", minHeight: 68, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 10,
                  fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
                }}
              />
              <input
                value={pending.why}
                onChange={(e) => setPending({ ...pending, why: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="why — optional, the incident behind it"
                style={{
                  width: "100%", padding: "9px 10px", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B",
                  fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
                }}
              />
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                <button onClick={commit} style={{ ...btnPrimary, padding: "9px 14px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>Save rule</button>
                <button onClick={() => { setPending(null); setError(null); }} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "9px 14px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>Discard</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {list.length >= 25 && (
            <div style={{ border: "2px solid #2F6B4F", background: "#E5EDE4", padding: "10px 12px", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
              {list.length} rules is a lot to hold at once. The sharp ones work better without the rest around them — worth pruning anything that has stopped being true.
            </div>
          )}

          <div style={{ borderTop: "2px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 12, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
            {list.length === 0 ? (
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                No rules yet — the chef is running on its general training alone.
              </div>
            ) : list.map((r) => (
              <div key={r.id} style={{ border: "2px solid #D8D0BF", padding: "10px 12px" }}>
                {editingId === r.id ? (
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
                    <textarea
                      value={r.text}
                      onChange={(e) => update(r.id, "text", e.target.value)}
                      style={{
                        width: "100%", minHeight: 64, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 9,
                        fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
                      }}
                    />
                    <input
                      value={r.why || ""}
                      onChange={(e) => update(r.id, "why", e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="why — optional"
                      style={{
                        width: "100%", padding: "8px 10px", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B",
                        fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
                      }}
                    />
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                      <button onClick={() => setEditingId(null)} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 12 }}>Done</button>
                      <button onClick={() => remove(r.id)} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 12, color: "#BE3B2A", borderColor: "#BE3B2A" }}>Delete rule</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <button onClick={() => setEditingId(r.id)} style={{
                    width: "100%", textAlign: "left", background: "none", border: "none", padding: 0, cursor: "pointer", display: "block",
                  }}>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                      {r.text}
                    </div>
                    {(r.why || "").trim() && (
                      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontStyle: "italic", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 5, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
                        {r.why}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// Where a recipe came from, shown at cook time rather than buried. A dish from
// a named cook says so on the card; a generated one says that too, so the
// difference stays visible instead of blurring.
function Provenance({ source, generated }) {
  const src = source || (generated ? { kind: "chef" } : null);
  if (!src) return null;
  if (src.kind === "chef") {
    return (
      <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, border: "1.5px solid #A9A192", color: "#6E6759", padding: "2px 6px", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
        Chef
      </span>
    );
  }
  return (
    <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, border: "1.5px solid #1B1B1B", color: "#1B1B1B", padding: "2px 6px", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
      {src.cook ? src.cook : "From a cook"}
    </span>
  );
}

function SourceBlock({ source }) {
  if (!source || source.kind === "chef") return null;
  const bits = [source.cook, source.site].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 14px" }}>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 5 }}>
        Recipe by
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
        {bits || "Unattributed"}
      </div>
      {(source.upstream || "").trim() && (
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 4, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
          Adapted by them from {source.upstream}
        </div>
      )}
      {(source.url || "").trim() && (
        <a href={source.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{
          // Going to someone else's site isn't your activity — links read as ink.
          display: "inline-block", marginTop: 8, fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#1B1B1B", textDecoration: "underline",
        }}>
          Open the original ↗
        </a>
      )}
      {Array.isArray(source.commentNotes) && source.commentNotes.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 10, borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 8 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 5 }}>
            What other cooks reported
          </div>
          {source.commentNotes.map((c, i) => (
            <div key={i} style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>· {c}</div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {Array.isArray(source.adaptations) && source.adaptations.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 10, borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 8 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 5 }}>
            Changed to fit this kitchen
          </div>
          {source.adaptations.map((a, i) => (
            <div key={i} style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>· {a}</div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// What changed and how it went, in the cook's words, dated. Distinct from the
// verdict, which is a judgement on the whole dish — these are the specifics,
// and they're what the chef learns the most from.
function EditsLog({ recipe, onAdd, onRemove }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const edits = Array.isArray(recipe.edits) ? recipe.edits : [];
  const add = () => {
    const body = text.trim();
    if (!body) return;
    onAdd(recipe.key, { id: "e" + uid(), date: localDateStr(), text: body });
    setText("");
  };
  return (
    <div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 7 }}>
        What you changed — the chef reads this
      </div>
      {edits.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6, marginBottom: 10 }}>
          {edits.map((e) => (
            <div key={e.id} style={{ border: "2px solid #D8D0BF", padding: "8px 10px", display: "flex", gap: 10, alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
              <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, color: "#6E6759", letterSpacing: 0.4 }}>{e.date}</div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6, marginTop: 3 }}>{e.text}</div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => onRemove(recipe.key, e.id)} title="Remove" style={{
                border: "none", background: "transparent", cursor: "pointer", padding: "0 2px",
                fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 14, color: "#A9A192",
              }}>×</button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <textarea
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="cut the soy sauce by half and it was right / doubled the tofu for the protein"
        style={{
          width: "100%", minHeight: 54, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 10,
          fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
        }}
      />
      <button onClick={add} style={{ ...btnGhost, marginTop: 8, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 12 }}>
        Add edit
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

// The loop that keeps this from being a silo. A recipe from a named cook, made
// and changed, is worth exactly the report the author asked for — so the app
// writes it out and hands it over rather than letting it die in local storage.
function ReportBack({ recipe }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const src = recipe.source;
  const edits = Array.isArray(recipe.edits) ? recipe.edits : [];
  if (!src || src.kind === "chef" || !(src.url || "").trim() || edits.length === 0) return null;

  const text = `Made this${recipe.lastMade ? ` (${recipe.lastMade})` : ""}. What I changed: ` +
    edits.map((e) => e.text.replace(/\s+$/, "").replace(/\.$/, "")).join("; ") + "." +
    (recipe.verdict === "loved" ? " Really good — will make again." :
     recipe.verdict === "never" ? " Didn't work out for me, but posting the changes in case it helps." : "");

  const copy = async () => {
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      } else {
        const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
        ta.value = text; ta.style.position = "fixed"; ta.style.opacity = "0";
        document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand("copy");
        document.body.removeChild(ta);
      }
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2200);
    } catch (e) {}
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ border: "2px solid #2F6B4F", background: "#E5EDE4", padding: "12px 14px" }}>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#2F6B4F", marginBottom: 6 }}>
        Report back
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
        You changed {src.cook ? `${src.cook}'s` : "this"} recipe and it worked out. Saying what you changed is what the comments are for — it's how the next person gets it right.
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontStyle: "italic", fontSize: 12, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6, marginTop: 8, borderLeft: "2px solid #2F6B4F", paddingLeft: 10 }}>
        {text}
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
        <button onClick={copy} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 12 }}>
          {copied ? "Copied" : "Copy it"}
        </button>
        <a href={src.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{
          ...btnGhost, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 12, textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex",
        }}>
          Go and post it ↗
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// Who this kitchen is learning from. A list of names and links rather than a
// closed drawer of recipes — the point is that it sends you back out.
function Cooks({ recipes }) {
  const cooks = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map();
    (recipes || []).forEach((r) => {
      const src = r.source;
      if (!src || src.kind === "chef" || !(src.cook || "").trim()) return;
      const name = src.cook.trim();
      const hit = map.get(name) || { name, site: src.site || "", url: src.url || "", count: 0 };
      hit.count += 1;
      if (!hit.url && src.url) hit.url = src.url;
      if (!hit.site && src.site) hit.site = src.site;
      map.set(name, hit);
    });
    return [...map.values()].sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);
  }, [recipes]);

  if (cooks.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div style={{ border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", background: "#F9F6EE", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", padding: 16 }}>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
        Cooking from
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 4, marginBottom: 12, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
        The people this kitchen is learning from.
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
        {cooks.map((c) => (
          <div key={c.name} style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 8, display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline", gap: 10 }}>
            <div>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{c.name}</div>
              {c.url ? (
                <a href={c.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#1B1B1B", textDecoration: "underline" }}>
                  {c.site || "open ↗"}
                </a>
              ) : c.site ? (
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759" }}>{c.site}</div>
              ) : null}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759" }}>
              {c.count} recipe{c.count === 1 ? "" : "s"}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ChefPanel({ recipes, onSave, seed, onSeedUsed, rules }) {
  const stashed = loadChefDraft();
  const [request, setRequest] = useState((stashed && stashed.request) || "");
  const [scope, setScope] = useState((stashed && stashed.scope) || "prep");
  const [angle, setAngle] = useState((stashed && stashed.angle) || "new");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState((stashed && stashed.draft) || null);
  const [restored, setRestored] = useState(!!stashed);
  const [showList, setShowList] = useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState("new");
  const [paste, setPaste] = useState("");
  const [showPaste, setShowPaste] = useState(false);
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [importServings, setImportServings] = useState("");
  const boxRef = React.useRef(null);

  const stash = (d, req, sc, an) => {
    if (d) lsSet(CHEF_DRAFT_KEY, JSON.stringify({ draft: d, request: req, scope: sc, angle: an }));
    else lsRemove(CHEF_DRAFT_KEY);
  };

  // A failed batch hands the chef the context directly, so the replacement
  // request never has to be retyped from scratch. On a phone the panel is
  // below the fold, so it comes to you rather than the other way round.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!seed) return;
    setRequest(seed.text || "");
    if (seed.scope) setScope(seed.scope);
    if (seed.angle) setAngle(seed.angle);
    setDraft(null);
    setRestored(false);
    setError(null);
    stash(null);
    if (boxRef.current && boxRef.current.scrollIntoView) {
      boxRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    }
    if (onSeedUsed) onSeedUsed();
  }, [seed]);

  const run = async () => {
    const body = request.trim();
    if (!body && angle === "onhand") {
      setError("Tell it what you actually have — a few ingredients is enough.");
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true); setError(null); setDraft(null); setRestored(false); setShowList(false);
    try {
      const ask = body || (scope === "meal"
        ? "Cook me one really good dinner tonight. Your call on what."
        : "Build me this week's prep. Your call on what.");
      const result = await askChef(ask, buildChefSystem(recipes, scope, angle, rules));
      setDraft(result);
      stash(result, body, scope, angle);
    } catch (e) {
      const msg = String(e.message || "");
      setError(msg === "no-key"
        ? "This one needs your Anthropic API key — the key icon up top. Everything else in the app works without it."
        : CHEF_ERRORS[msg] || `Could not reach the chef: ${msg}`);
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  // Bringing someone else's recipe in. One link is the whole input: the page is
  // read server-side, and the author, the publication, whoever they adapted it
  // from, and anything useful buried in the comments all come off the page
  // rather than being typed in by hand. The paste box stays as a fallback for
  // pages the fetcher can't read — anything rendered by JavaScript, or behind a
  // login.
  const runImport = async () => {
    const link = url.trim();
    const body = paste.trim();
    if (!link && !body) { setError("Paste the link to the recipe."); return; }
    setBusy(true); setError(null); setDraft(null); setRestored(false); setShowList(false);
    const want = Number(importServings) || (scope === "meal" ? 1 : 5);
    try {
      let result;
      if (link) {
        const ask = `Fetch this recipe page and bring the recipe into this kitchen, scaled to ${want} serving${want === 1 ? "" : "s"}: ${link}`;
        result = await askChefUrl(ask, buildUrlImportSystem(recipes, rules), link);
      } else {
        const ask = `Convert this recipe for ${want} serving${want === 1 ? "" : "s"}.\n\n${body}`;
        result = await askChef(ask, buildImportSystem(recipes, rules));
      }
      if (result.error) { setError(`The chef couldn't find a recipe there — ${result.error}.`); setBusy(false); return; }
      const withSource = {
        ...result,
        scope: scope === "meal" ? "meal" : "prep",
        servings: want,
        source: {
          kind: "cook",
          cook: (result.cook || "").trim(),
          site: (result.site || "").trim(),
          url: link,
          upstream: (result.upstream || "").trim(),
          adaptations: Array.isArray(result.adaptations) ? result.adaptations.filter(Boolean) : [],
          commentNotes: Array.isArray(result.commentNotes) ? result.commentNotes.filter(Boolean) : [],
        },
      };
      setDraft(withSource);
      stash(withSource, body, scope, angle);
    } catch (e) {
      const msg = String(e.message || "");
      setError(
        msg === "no-key"
          ? "This one needs your Anthropic API key — the key icon up top."
          : msg.startsWith("fetch:")
            ? `${FETCH_ERRORS[msg.slice(6)] || "That page couldn't be read."} You can paste the recipe text in instead.`
            : CHEF_ERRORS[msg] || `Could not reach the chef: ${msg}`
      );
      if (msg.startsWith("fetch:") || msg === "truncated") setShowPaste(true);
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  const activeHint = (ANGLE_LABELS.find((a) => a.key === angle) || {}).hint;
  const placeholder = angle === "onhand"
    ? (scope === "meal"
      ? "a fennel bulb, half a bag of carrots, two zucchini, an onion"
      : "fresh vegetables to use up: fennel, carrots, zucchini, a bag of spinach")
    : (scope === "meal"
      ? "something warm, I have about 40 minutes — or leave this blank"
      : "leave blank and let the chef choose, or steer it: no rice this week");

  return (
    <div ref={boxRef} style={{ border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", background: "#F9F6EE", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #75695A", scrollMarginTop: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ background: "var(--activity)", color: "#F9F6EE", padding: "12px 16px" }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
          Ask the chef
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.9, marginTop: 2, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
          It already knows your oven, your storage, your protein target, and everything you have told it didn't work.
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{ padding: 16, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 14 }}>

        <div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 7 }}>
            Where it comes from
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            <Chip active={mode === "new"} onClick={() => { setMode("new"); setError(null); }}>The chef invents it</Chip>
            <Chip active={mode === "import"} onClick={() => { setMode("import"); setError(null); }}>Bring in a cook's recipe</Chip>
          </div>
          {mode === "import" && (
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 7, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              The chef fits it to this kitchen and says what it changed. The recipe stays theirs.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 7 }}>
            Cooking what
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
            <Chip active={scope === "prep"} onClick={() => setScope("prep")}>A week of lunches</Chip>
            <Chip active={scope === "meal"} onClick={() => setScope("meal")}>One meal tonight</Chip>
          </div>
        </div>

        {mode === "import" && (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
            <input
              value={url}
              onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="paste the link to the recipe"
              inputMode="url"
              style={importField}
            />
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              The chef reads the page — who wrote it, who they adapted it from, and what the comments say went wrong for people.
            </div>
            <input
              value={importServings}
              onChange={(e) => setImportServings(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="servings — blank uses the scope default"
              inputMode="numeric"
              style={importField}
            />

            {showPaste ? (
              <textarea
                value={paste}
                onChange={(e) => setPaste(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="paste the recipe text here instead"
                style={{
                  width: "100%", minHeight: 130, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 10,
                  fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
                }}
              />
            ) : (
              <button onClick={() => setShowPaste(true)} style={{
                ...btnGhost, padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", border: "2px solid #D8D0BF", alignSelf: "flex-start",
              }}>
                No link? Paste the text instead
              </button>
            )}

            <button onClick={runImport} disabled={busy} style={{
              ...btnPrimary, opacity: busy ? 0.6 : 1, justifyContent: "center", padding: "12px 16px",
            }}>
              {busy ? "Reading the page…" : "Bring it in"}
            </button>
            {error && (
              <div style={{ border: "2px solid #BE3B2A", background: "#F9E4DE", padding: 12, fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                {error}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {mode === "new" && (
        <>
        <div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 7 }}>
            Starting point
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            {ANGLE_LABELS.map((a) => (
              <Chip key={a.key} active={angle === a.key} onClick={() => setAngle(a.key)}>{a.label}</Chip>
            ))}
          </div>
          {activeHint && (
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 7, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
              {activeHint}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <textarea
          value={request}
          onChange={(e) => setRequest(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          style={{
            width: "100%", minHeight: 70, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 10,
            fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
          }}
        />
        <button onClick={run} disabled={busy} style={{ ...btnPrimary, opacity: busy ? 0.6 : 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>
          {busy ? "Thinking…" : scope === "meal" ? "Build me a meal" : "Build me a prep"}
        </button>

        {error && (
          <div style={{ border: "2px solid #BE3B2A", background: "#F9E4DE", padding: 12, fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
        </>
        )}

        {draft && (
          <div style={{ borderTop: "2px solid #1B1B1B", paddingTop: 14, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ background: restored ? "#FFC400" : "#E7E0D0", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "8px 12px", fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
              {restored ? "Recovered — still not saved" : "Not saved yet"}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.4, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.15 }}>
              {draft.name}
            </div>

            {draft.source && draft.source.kind === "cook" && (
              <div style={{ border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 12, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>
                  Read off the page — fix it if it's wrong
                </div>
                <input
                  value={draft.source.cook || ""}
                  onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, source: { ...draft.source, cook: e.target.value } })}
                  placeholder="whose recipe is it"
                  style={importField}
                />
                <input
                  value={draft.source.site || ""}
                  onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, source: { ...draft.source, site: e.target.value } })}
                  placeholder="where it's published"
                  style={importField}
                />
                {(draft.source.upstream || "").trim() && (
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
                    They adapted it from {draft.source.upstream}
                  </div>
                )}
                {Array.isArray(draft.source.commentNotes) && draft.source.commentNotes.length > 0 && (
                  <div style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 8 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 5 }}>
                      What the comments say
                    </div>
                    {draft.source.commentNotes.map((c, i) => (
                      <div key={i} style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>· {c}</div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11 }}>
              <span style={{ background: "var(--activity)", color: "#F9F6EE", padding: "2px 6px" }}>{draft.proteinG}g protein</span>
              <span style={{ color: "#6E6759", marginLeft: 8 }}>{draft.activeMinutes} min active</span>
              {draft.cuisine && <span style={{ color: "#6E6759", marginLeft: 8 }}>{draft.cuisine}</span>}
            </div>
            {draft.technique && (
              <div style={{ background: "#FBEFCB", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 14px", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, lineHeight: 1.6, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
                <strong>Technique:</strong> {draft.technique}
              </div>
            )}
            {(draft.ingredients || []).length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, color: "#6E6759", letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  Ingredients
                </div>
                {draft.ingredients.map((ing, i) => (
                  <div key={i} style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.7, borderBottom: "1px solid #E2DACA", padding: "3px 0" }}>
                    {ing}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
            {(draft.method || []).map((m, i) => (
              <div key={i} style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 8 }}>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, color: "#6E6759", letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
                  Step {i + 1}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 2 }}>{m.t}</div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6, marginTop: 2 }}>{m.d}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
            {hasGrocery(draft) && (
              <>
                <button onClick={() => setShowList(!showList)} style={{ ...btnGhost, justifyContent: "center", fontSize: 12.5, padding: "10px 14px" }}>
                  {showList ? "Hide shopping list" : "Shopping list"}
                </button>
                {showList && <GroceryList recipe={draft} />}
              </>
            )}
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <button onClick={() => { onSave(draft, scope); setDraft(null); setRestored(false); setRequest(""); setPaste(""); setUrl(""); setShowPaste(false); setShowList(false); stash(null); }} style={{ ...btnPrimary, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>
                Save to rotation
              </button>
              <button onClick={run} disabled={busy} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: "0 0 auto", justifyContent: "center" }}>
                Retry
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => { setDraft(null); setRestored(false); setShowList(false); stash(null); }} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: "0 0 auto", justifyContent: "center" }}>
                Discard
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ---------- kitchen tab ----------
// A day is not a checkbox. The prep gets eaten, or something else does, or
// lunch just doesn't happen — and all three are worth knowing.
const DAY_CYCLE = ["", "prep", "other", "skip"];
const DAY_STYLE = {
  prep: { background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", border: "2px solid #F9F6EE" },
  other: { background: "#FFC400", color: "#1B1B1B", border: "2px solid #FFC400" },
  skip: { background: "transparent", color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.35)", border: "2px dashed rgba(255,255,255,0.4)" },
};

const FAIL_REASONS = ["Texture was wrong", "Bland", "Didn't keep", "Too much work", "Just didn't want it"];

function KitchenTab({ recipes, setRecipes, mealPlan, setMealPlan, sessions, rules, setRules }) {
  const [openKey, setOpenKey] = useState(null);
  const [showList, setShowList] = useState(null);
  const [failOpen, setFailOpen] = useState(false);
  const [failReason, setFailReason] = useState("");
  const [failNote, setFailNote] = useState("");
  const [seed, setSeed] = useState(null);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("all");
  const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState(null);
  const today = localDateStr();
  const thisWeek = prepWeekOf(today);
  const currentKey = recipeForWeek(mealPlan, today);
  const current = recipes.find((r) => r.key === currentKey);
  const entry = (mealPlan || []).find((m) => m.weekStart === thisWeek) || {};

  // Older weeks stored a flat list of dates that were eaten. Read those as
  // "ate the prep" so no history is lost when the richer states arrive.
  const days = entry.days || Object.fromEntries(((entry.eaten) || []).map((d) => [d, "prep"]));

  const assignWeek = (key) => {
    setMealPlan((prev) => {
      const rest = (prev || []).filter((m) => m.weekStart !== thisWeek);
      return [...rest, { weekStart: thisWeek, recipeKey: key, days: {} }];
    });
    setFailOpen(false);
  };

  // Assigning a week is a plan; lastMade is a fact. Stamping the date on assign
  // marked recipes as cooked that had only been queued up.
  const markMade = (key) =>
    setRecipes((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.key === key ? { ...r, lastMade: today } : r)));

  // A generated recipe becomes a first-class card: same shape, same storage,
  // so it progresses and gets grocery-listed like anything else.
  const addEdit = (key, edit) => {
    setRecipes((prev) => prev.map((r) => (
      r.key === key ? { ...r, edits: [...(Array.isArray(r.edits) ? r.edits : []), edit] } : r
    )));
  };
  const removeEdit = (key, editId) => {
    setRecipes((prev) => prev.map((r) => (
      r.key === key ? { ...r, edits: (Array.isArray(r.edits) ? r.edits : []).filter((e) => e.id !== editId) } : r
    )));
  };

  const saveGenerated = (draft, scope) => {
    const key = "gen" + Date.now();
    setRecipes((prev) => [...prev, {
      key,
      name: draft.name || "Untitled recipe",
      scope: draft.scope === "meal" || scope === "meal" ? "meal" : "prep",
      servings: Number(draft.servings) || (scope === "meal" ? 1 : 5),
      cuisine: draft.cuisine || "",
      protein: draft.protein || "", grain: draft.grain || "",
      veg: draft.veg || "", sauce: draft.sauce || "",
      proteinG: Number(draft.proteinG) || 0,
      fiberG: Number(draft.fiberG) || 0,
      activeMinutes: Number(draft.activeMinutes) || 0,
      prepNotes: draft.prepNotes || "",
      technique: draft.technique || "",
      ingredients: Array.isArray(draft.ingredients) ? draft.ingredients.filter(Boolean) : [],
      method: Array.isArray(draft.method) ? draft.method : [],
      grocery: normalizeGrocery(draft.grocery),
      generated: true,
      outcome: "",
      verdict: null,
      verdictNote: "",
      lastMade: null,
      edits: [],
      source: draft.source || { kind: "chef" },
    }]);
    setOpenKey(key);
    // The read-back check that used to live here now runs inside every write,
    // so a refused save is caught for recipes and everything else alike.
  };

  const deleteRecipe = (key) => {
    setRecipes((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.key !== key));
    setMealPlan((prev) => (prev || []).filter((m) => m.recipeKey !== key));
    setOpenKey(null);
    setConfirmDelete(null);
  };

  // One tap moves a day forward through the cycle. Nothing here should ever
  // take more than a tap, or it won't get logged at all.
  const cycleDay = (dateStr) => {
    setMealPlan((prev) => (prev || []).map((m) => {
      if (m.weekStart !== thisWeek) return m;
      const cur = (m.days || Object.fromEntries(((m.eaten) || []).map((d) => [d, "prep"]))) || {};
      const next = DAY_CYCLE[(DAY_CYCLE.indexOf(cur[dateStr] || "") + 1) % DAY_CYCLE.length];
      const updated = { ...cur };
      if (next) updated[dateStr] = next; else delete updated[dateStr];
      const { eaten, ...rest } = m;
      return { ...rest, days: updated };
    }));
  };

  const setWeekNote = (text) => {
    setMealPlan((prev) => (prev || []).map((m) => (m.weekStart === thisWeek ? { ...m, note: text } : m)));
  };

  const setVerdict = (key, verdict, note) =>
    setRecipes((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.key === key
      ? { ...r, verdict, verdictNote: note !== undefined ? note : (r.verdictNote || "") }
      : r)));

  const setOutcome = (key, text) =>
    setRecipes((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.key === key ? { ...r, outcome: text } : r)));

  // Recording the failure and asking for the replacement are the same gesture.
  const recordFailure = (permanent) => {
    if (!current) return;
    const note = [failReason, failNote.trim()].filter(Boolean).join(" — ");
    setVerdict(current.key, permanent ? "never" : "ok", note);
    setSeed({
      scope: "prep",
      angle: "new",
      text: `Replace this week's prep. I cooked "${current.name}" and it didn't work: ${note || "it just wasn't good"}. Build something genuinely different that avoids that problem.`,
    });
    setFailOpen(false);
    setFailReason("");
    setFailNote("");
  };

  const daysSince = (d) => {
    if (!d) return null;
    const [y, m, dd] = d.split("-").map(Number);
    const t = new Date(); t.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    return Math.round((t - new Date(y, m - 1, dd)) / 86400000);
  };

  const counts = Object.values(days).reduce((acc, v) => { acc[v] = (acc[v] || 0) + 1; return acc; }, {});
  const cooked = recipes.filter((r) => r.lastMade);
  const cuisines = Array.from(new Set(cooked.map((r) => r.cuisine).filter(Boolean)));
  const techniques = cooked.filter((r) => r.technique).length;
  const shown = recipes.filter((r) => filter === "all" || (filter === "meal" ? r.scope === "meal" : r.scope !== "meal"));

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>
      <div style={{ background: "#1B1B1B", color: "#F9F6EE", padding: 22, boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #75695A" }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: 1.4, textTransform: "uppercase", opacity: 0.65 }}>
          This week
        </div>
        {current ? (
          <>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.6, margin: "6px 0 8px", lineHeight: 1.1 }}>
              {current.name}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11.5 }}>
              <span style={{ background: "#FFC400", color: "#1B1B1B", padding: "2px 6px" }}>{current.proteinG}g protein</span>
              <span style={{ opacity: 0.75, marginLeft: 8 }}>{current.protein} · {current.grain}</span>
            </div>

            <div style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1.2, textTransform: "uppercase", opacity: 0.6, marginBottom: 8 }}>
                {(counts.prep || 0)} prep · {(counts.other || 0)} something else · {(counts.skip || 0)} skipped
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6 }}>
                {weekDays(thisWeek).map((d) => {
                  const state = days[d.date] || "";
                  const isToday = d.date === today;
                  const st = DAY_STYLE[state] || { background: "transparent", color: "#F9F6EE", border: "2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)" };
                  return (
                    <button key={d.date} onClick={() => cycleDay(d.date)} style={{
                      flex: 1, padding: "10px 0", cursor: "pointer",
                      background: st.background, color: st.color,
                      border: isToday ? "2px solid #FFC400" : st.border,
                      fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                    }}>{d.label}</button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.55, marginTop: 7, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                Tap a day to cycle: ate the prep → ate something else → skipped.
              </div>

              <input
                value={entry.note || ""}
                onChange={(e) => setWeekNote(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="optional — what happened this week"
                style={{
                  width: "100%", marginTop: 10, padding: "9px 10px", borderRadius: 0,
                  border: "2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.35)", background: "transparent", color: "#F9F6EE",
                  fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5,
                }}
              />

              {!failOpen ? (
                <button onClick={() => setFailOpen(true)} style={{
                  ...btnGhost, marginTop: 12, width: "100%", justifyContent: "center",
                  border: "2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)", color: "#F9F6EE", fontSize: 12.5, padding: "10px 14px",
                }}>
                  This batch didn't work
                </button>
              ) : (
                <div style={{ marginTop: 12, border: "2px solid #FFC400", padding: 14 }}>
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", opacity: 0.7, marginBottom: 8 }}>
                    What went wrong
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                    {FAIL_REASONS.map((f) => (
                      <button key={f} onClick={() => setFailReason(failReason === f ? "" : f)} style={{
                        padding: "8px 12px", borderRadius: 0, cursor: "pointer",
                        border: `2px solid ${failReason === f ? "#FFC400" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.4)"}`,
                        background: failReason === f ? "#FFC400" : "transparent",
                        color: failReason === f ? "#1B1B1B" : "#F9F6EE",
                        fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: 500,
                      }}>{f}</button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                  <input
                    value={failNote}
                    onChange={(e) => setFailNote(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="optional detail"
                    style={{
                      width: "100%", marginTop: 10, padding: "9px 10px", borderRadius: 0,
                      border: "2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.35)", background: "transparent", color: "#F9F6EE",
                      fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5,
                    }}
                  />
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                    <button onClick={() => recordFailure(true)} style={{
                      ...btnPrimary, background: "#BE3B2A", color: "#F9F6EE", flex: 1, justifyContent: "center", fontSize: 12.5, padding: "10px 12px",
                    }}>
                      Never again
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={() => recordFailure(false)} style={{
                      ...btnGhost, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center", border: "2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)", color: "#F9F6EE", fontSize: 12.5, padding: "10px 12px",
                    }}>
                      Fix it next time
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.6, marginTop: 8, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                    Either way the chef is told, and a replacement request gets written for you.
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, opacity: 0.8, marginTop: 8, lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            No prep assigned yet. Pick one below and tap "Cooking this week."
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <ChefPanel recipes={recipes} onSave={saveGenerated} seed={seed} onSeedUsed={() => setSeed(null)} rules={rules} />

      <KitchenRules rules={rules} setRules={setRules} />

      <Cooks recipes={recipes} />

      <div style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", padding: 18 }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
          Horizons
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", marginTop: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 12px", borderRight: "2px solid #1B1B1B" }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 9.5, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>Dishes cooked</div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 38, fontWeight: 900, letterSpacing: -2, lineHeight: 1, color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 3 }}>{cooked.length}</div>
          </div>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 12px" }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 9.5, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>Techniques learned</div>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 38, fontWeight: 900, letterSpacing: -2, lineHeight: 1, color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 3 }}>{techniques}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        {cuisines.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 8, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
            {cuisines.join(" · ")}
          </div>
        )}
        <button
          onClick={() => setSeed({ scope: "prep", angle: "new", text: "" })}
          style={{ ...btnPrimary, marginTop: 12, width: "100%", justifyContent: "center", fontSize: 13 }}
        >
          Take me somewhere new
        </button>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 8, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
          The chef sees everything you have already cooked and is told to stay off that ground.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B", flex: 1 }}>
          Rotation
        </div>
        {[["all", "All"], ["prep", "Preps"], ["meal", "Meals"]].map(([k, label]) => (
          <button key={k} onClick={() => setFilter(k)} style={{
            padding: "6px 10px", borderRadius: 0, cursor: "pointer",
            border: `2px solid ${filter === k ? "#1B1B1B" : "#D8D0BF"}`,
            background: filter === k ? "#1B1B1B" : "#F9F6EE",
            color: filter === k ? "#F9F6EE" : "#6E6759",
            fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.6,
          }}>{label}</button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {shown.map((r) => {
        const open = openKey === r.key;
        const since = daysSince(r.lastMade);
        const isMeal = r.scope === "meal";
        return (
          <div key={r.key} style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", opacity: r.verdict === "never" ? 0.55 : 1 }}>
            <button onClick={() => setOpenKey(open ? null : r.key)} style={{
              width: "100%", textAlign: "left", background: "none", border: "none",
              padding: 16, cursor: "pointer", display: "block",
            }}>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.2, letterSpacing: -0.3 }}>
                {r.name}
              </div>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 6, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>
                {r.protein} · {r.grain}{r.cuisine ? ` · ${r.cuisine}` : ""}
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, marginTop: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center" }}>
                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, background: "var(--activity)", color: "#F9F6EE", padding: "2px 6px" }}>
                  {r.proteinG}g protein
                </span>
                {isMeal && (
                  <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, border: "1.5px solid #1B1B1B", color: "#1B1B1B", padding: "2px 6px", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
                    Single meal
                  </span>
                )}
                <Provenance source={r.source} generated={r.generated} />
                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759" }}>
                  {since == null ? "not yet made" : since === 0 ? "made today" : `${since}d ago`}
                </span>
                {r.key === currentKey && (
                  <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, background: "#FFC400", color: "#1B1B1B", padding: "2px 6px", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
                    This week
                  </span>
                )}
                {r.verdict === "loved" && (
                  <span style={{ ...stampStyle, borderColor: "#1B1B1B", color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
                    Loved it
                  </span>
                )}
                {r.verdict === "never" && (
                  <span style={{ ...stampStyle, borderColor: "#BE3B2A", color: "#BE3B2A" }}>
                    Never again
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              {(r.verdictNote || r.outcome) && !open && (
                r.verdictNote
                  ? <div style={annotationStyle}>{r.verdictNote}</div>
                  : <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontStyle: "italic", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 9, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>{r.outcome}</div>
              )}
            </button>

            {open && (
              <div style={{ borderTop: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 16, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 14 }}>
                <SourceBlock source={r.source} />

                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                  <strong>Veg:</strong> {r.veg}<br />
                  <strong>Sauce:</strong> {r.sauce}
                </div>

                <div style={{ background: "var(--activity-tint)", border: "2px solid var(--activity)", padding: "10px 14px", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                  <strong>Prep:</strong> {r.prepNotes}
                </div>

                {r.technique && (
                  <div style={{ background: "#FBEFCB", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 14px", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
                    <strong>Technique:</strong> {r.technique}
                  </div>
                )}

                {(r.ingredients || []).length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 6 }}>
                      Ingredients{r.servings ? ` · ${r.servings} serving${r.servings === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : ""}
                    </div>
                    {r.ingredients.map((ing, i) => (
                      <div key={i} style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.7, borderBottom: "1px solid #E2DACA", padding: "3px 0" }}>
                        {ing}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                {(r.method || []).length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                      Method{r.activeMinutes ? ` · ${r.activeMinutes} min active` : ""}
                    </div>
                    {r.method.map((m, i) => (
                      <div key={i} style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}>
                        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                          <span style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 800, color: "var(--activity)", minWidth: 18 }}>{i + 1}</span>
                          <div>
