                            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{m.t}</div>
                            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6, marginTop: 2 }}>{m.d}</div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B" }}>
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 12px", borderRight: "2px solid #1B1B1B" }}>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 9.5, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>Protein</div>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 38, fontWeight: 900, letterSpacing: -2, lineHeight: 1, color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 3 }}>{r.proteinG}<span style={{ fontSize: 14, letterSpacing: 0 }}>g</span></div>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 12px" }}>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 9.5, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759" }}>Fiber</div>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 38, fontWeight: 900, letterSpacing: -2, lineHeight: 1, color: "#1B1B1B", marginTop: 3 }}>{r.fiberG}<span style={{ fontSize: 14, letterSpacing: 0 }}>g</span></div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: -8, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                  Per serving, estimated from standard ingredient values for {isMeal ? "a single portion" : "a 5-lunch prep"}.
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#6E6759", marginBottom: 7 }}>
                    Verdict — the chef reads this
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                    <Chip active={r.verdict === "loved"} onClick={() => setVerdict(r.key, r.verdict === "loved" ? null : "loved")}>Loved it</Chip>
                    <Chip active={r.verdict === "ok"} onClick={() => setVerdict(r.key, r.verdict === "ok" ? null : "ok")}>Fine</Chip>
                    <Chip active={r.verdict === "never"} onClick={() => setVerdict(r.key, r.verdict === "never" ? null : "never")} color="#BE3B2A">Never again</Chip>
                  </div>
                  <textarea
                    value={r.outcome || ""}
                    onChange={(e) => setOutcome(r.key, e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="optional — kale held all 5 days / sauce needed acid"
                    style={{
                      width: "100%", minHeight: 54, marginTop: 10, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 10,
                      fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
                    }}
                  />
                </div>

                <EditsLog recipe={r} onAdd={addEdit} onRemove={removeEdit} />

                <ReportBack recipe={r} />

                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                  {!isMeal && (
                    <button onClick={() => assignWeek(r.key)} style={{ ...btnPrimary, padding: "10px 14px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>
                      Cooking this week
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button onClick={() => markMade(r.key)} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "10px 14px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>
                    {r.lastMade === today ? "Made today" : "Made it today"}
                  </button>
                  {hasGrocery(r) && (
                    <button onClick={() => setShowList(showList === r.key ? null : r.key)} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "10px 14px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>
                      {showList === r.key ? "Hide list" : "Shopping list"}
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setSeed({
                      scope: isMeal ? "meal" : "prep",
                      angle: "new",
                      text: `Something in the same spirit as "${r.name}" but genuinely different — a technique or cuisine I haven't cooked yet.`,
                    })}
                    style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "10px 14px", fontSize: 12.5 }}
                  >
                    Something like this, but new
                  </button>
                </div>

                {r.generated && (
                  confirmDelete === r.key ? (
                    <div style={{ border: "2px solid #BE3B2A", background: "#F9E4DE", padding: 12, display: "flex", gap: 8, alignItems: "center", flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                      <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", flex: 1, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                        Delete this recipe for good?
                      </div>
                      <button onClick={() => deleteRecipe(r.key)} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "#BE3B2A", padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 12 }}>Delete</button>
                      <button onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(null)} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 12 }}>Keep</button>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <button onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(r.key)} style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", border: "2px solid #D8D0BF", alignSelf: "flex-start" }}>
                      Delete recipe
                    </button>
                  )
                )}

                {showList === r.key && <GroceryList recipe={r} />}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}

      <div style={{ background: "#FBEFCB", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 18 }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#1B1B1B", marginBottom: 10 }}>
          Standing prep rules
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 7 }}>
          {PREP_FLAGS.map((f, i) => (
            <div key={i} style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              — {f}
            </div>
          ))}
          {recipes.filter((r) => r.verdict === "never").map((r) => (
            <div key={r.key} style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              — {r.name}: never again{r.verdictNote ? ` (${r.verdictNote})` : ""}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// A small square button for the corner of a history card. Deliberately not a
// Chip: these are destructive-adjacent controls that sit next to the reading
// matter, so they stay quiet until they're the active one.
const cardIconBtn = (activeColor) => ({
  width: 28, height: 28, borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #D8D0BF", background: "#F9F6EE",
  display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", cursor: "pointer",
  color: activeColor || "#6E6759", flex: "0 0 auto", padding: 0,
});

// A logged session is a record, not a receipt. A number mis-tapped on the gym
// floor doesn't just sit there looking wrong — the coach reads the last logged
// weight to decide what to recommend next time, so a stray 0 sets the ladder
// back to the bottom. This is the way back into one.
//
// The editor holds a local draft and writes only on Save. Committing on every
// stepper press would mean a dozen separate writes to storage for one
// correction, and no way to back out halfway through.
function SessionEditor({ session, exercises, onSave, onCancel }) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(session);
  const modalityCfg = MODALITIES.find((m) => m.key === draft.modality) || MODALITIES[0];
  const logged = Object.keys(draft.exercises || {});

  const setField = (field, v) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [field]: v }));
  const setEx = (key, field, v) => setDraft((d) => ({
    ...d,
    exercises: { ...(d.exercises || {}), [key]: { ...((d.exercises || {})[key] || {}), [field]: v } },
  }));
  const dropEx = (key) => setDraft((d) => {
    const next = { ...(d.exercises || {}) };
    delete next[key];
    return { ...d, exercises: next };
  });

  const fieldInput = {
    width: "100%", border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: "9px 12px",
    fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 14, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ marginTop: 14, borderTop: "2px solid #1B1B1B", paddingTop: 16, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 18 }}>
      <Field label="Date">
        <input
          type="date"
          value={draft.date}
          max={localDateStr()}
          onChange={(ev) => ev.target.value && setField("date", ev.target.value)}
          style={fieldInput}
        />
      </Field>

      <Field label="Duration (min : sec)">
        <StepperPair>
          <Stepper value={Math.floor(draft.duration || 0)} step={1} unit="min" min={0}
            onChange={(v) => setField("duration", v + ((draft.duration || 0) % 1))} />
          <Stepper value={Math.round(((draft.duration || 0) % 1) * 60)} step={15} unit="sec" min={0}
            onChange={(v) => {
              const mins = Math.floor(draft.duration || 0);
              setField("duration", v >= 60 ? mins + 1 : mins + v / 60);
            }} />
        </StepperPair>
      </Field>

      <Field label={`Distance (${modalityCfg.unit})`}>
        <Stepper value={draft.distance || 0} step={modalityCfg.step} unit={modalityCfg.unit} min={0}
          onChange={(v) => setField("distance", modalityCfg.unit === "yd" ? Math.round(v) : Math.round(v * 100) / 100)} />
      </Field>

      <Field label="Heart rate">
        <StepperPair>
          <Stepper value={draft.hrAvg} step={1} unit="avg" min={0} onChange={(v) => setField("hrAvg", v)} />
          <Stepper value={draft.hrMax} step={1} unit="max" min={0} onChange={(v) => setField("hrMax", v)} />
        </StepperPair>
      </Field>

      {logged.length === 0 && (
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#6E6759", textAlign: "center", lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
          No lifts recorded in this session. Only what was logged at the time can be corrected here.
        </div>
      )}

      {logged.map((k) => {
        const cfg = exercises[k] || { label: k, unit: "lb", step: 5 };
        const v = (draft.exercises || {})[k] || {};
        return (
          <div key={k} style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingTop: 14, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 14 }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 15, color: "#1B1B1B", textAlign: "center" }}>
              {cfg.label}
            </div>
            {v.weight != null && (
              <Field label="Weight used">
                <Stepper value={v.weight} step={cfg.step || 5} unit={cfg.unit || "lb"} min={0}
                  onChange={(n) => setEx(k, "weight", n)} />
              </Field>
            )}
            <Field label={v.sets != null ? "Sets × Reps" : "Reps"}>
              <StepperPair>
                {v.sets != null && <Stepper value={v.sets} step={1} unit="sets" min={1} onChange={(n) => setEx(k, "sets", n)} />}
                <Stepper value={v.reps} step={1} unit="reps" min={1} onChange={(n) => setEx(k, "reps", n)} />
              </StepperPair>
            </Field>
            <Field label="How'd it feel?">
              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
                {FEEL_OPTIONS.map((f) => (
                  <Chip key={f.key} active={v.feel === f.key} color={f.color} onClick={() => setEx(k, "feel", f.key)}>
                    {f.label}
                  </Chip>
                ))}
              </div>
            </Field>
            <button
              onClick={() => dropEx(k)}
              style={{ ...btnGhost, width: "100%", justifyContent: "center", padding: "9px 12px", fontSize: 12.5, borderColor: "#BE3B2A", color: "#BE3B2A" }}
            >
              Remove {cfg.label} from this session
            </button>
          </div>
        );
      })}

      <Field label="Notes">
        <textarea
          value={draft.notes || ""}
          onChange={(ev) => setField("notes", ev.target.value)}
          placeholder="Optional — form notes, how it felt, etc."
          style={{
            width: "100%", minHeight: 70, borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B",
            padding: 10, fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 14, resize: "vertical",
            background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B",
          }}
        />
      </Field>

      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
        <button onClick={onCancel} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>Cancel</button>
        <button onClick={() => onSave(draft)} style={{ ...btnPrimary, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>
          <Check size={16} /> Save changes
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function HistoryTab({ sessions, exercises, allStretches = STRETCHES, recipes = [], mealPlan = [], onUpdateSession, onDeleteSession }) {
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null);
  const [confirmId, setConfirmId] = useState(null);

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
      {[...sessions].reverse().map((s) => {
        const editing = editingId === s.id;
        return (
        <div key={s.id} style={{ background: "#F9F6EE", border: "2.5px solid #1B1B1B", boxShadow: "6px 6px 0 #1B1B1B", borderRadius: 0, padding: "14px 18px" }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 10 }}>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Archivo', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 15, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
              {s.date} {s.type && s.type !== "logged" && <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759" }}>· {s.type}</span>}
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, flex: "0 0 auto" }}>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12, color: "var(--activity)" }}>{s.distance ? `${s.distance} mi` : ""}</div>
              {onUpdateSession && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setEditingId(editing ? null : s.id); setConfirmId(null); }}
                  aria-label="Edit session"
                  title="Edit session"
                  style={cardIconBtn(editing ? "var(--activity)" : null)}
                >
                  <Pencil size={13} />
                </button>
              )}
              {onDeleteSession && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setConfirmId(confirmId === s.id ? null : s.id); setEditingId(null); }}
                  aria-label="Delete session"
                  title="Delete session"
                  style={cardIconBtn(confirmId === s.id ? "#BE3B2A" : null)}
                >
                  <X size={13} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {!editing && (
            <React.Fragment>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 4 }}>
                {s.duration}min · HR {s.hrAvg}/{s.hrMax}
              </div>
              {s.exercises && Object.keys(s.exercises).length > 0 && (
                <div style={{ marginTop: 8, display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 6 }}>
                  {Object.entries(s.exercises).map(([k, v]) => (
                    <span key={k} style={{
                      fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11, padding: "4px 8px",
                      borderRadius: 0, background: "#D8D0BF", color: "#1B1B1B",
                    }}>
                      {exercises[k]?.label || k}: {v.weight ?? 0}{exercises[k]?.unit}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
              {(() => {
                const done = allStretches.filter((cfg) => normalizeStretchRounds((s.stretchRounds || {})[cfg.key], cfg) > 0);
                if (done.length === 0) return null;
                const total = done.reduce((sum, cfg) => sum + stretchAccum(cfg, normalizeStretchRounds(s.stretchRounds[cfg.key], cfg)), 0);
                return (
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 6 }}>
                    Stretch: {done.length} movement{done.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} · {total}s total
                  </div>
                );
              })()}
              {(() => {
                const key = recipeForWeek(mealPlan, s.date);
                const r = key && recipes.find((x) => x.key === key);
                if (!r) return null;
                return (
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10.5, marginTop: 6 }}>
                    <span style={{ background: "#FFC400", color: "#1B1B1B", padding: "2px 6px", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
                      {r.protein} week
                    </span>
                    <span style={{ color: "#6E6759", marginLeft: 6 }}>{r.proteinG}g protein/lunch</span>
                  </div>
                );
              })()}
              {s.notes && <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 8, fontStyle: "italic" }}>{s.notes}</div>}
            </React.Fragment>
          )}

          {confirmId === s.id && (
            <div style={{ marginTop: 10, border: "2px solid #BE3B2A", background: "#F9E4DE", padding: "10px 12px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: 600, color: "#BE3B2A", lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                Delete this whole session? The run, the lifts and the notes all go with it, and there's no undo.
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                <button onClick={() => setConfirmId(null)} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center", padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>
                  Keep it
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => { setConfirmId(null); onDeleteSession(s.id); }} style={{ ...btnPrimary, background: "#BE3B2A", flex: 1, justifyContent: "center", padding: "8px 12px", fontSize: 12.5 }}>
                  Delete
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {editing && (
            <SessionEditor
              key={s.id}
              session={s}
              exercises={exercises}
              onCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
              onSave={(next) => { setEditingId(null); onUpdateSession(s.id, next); }}
            />
          )}
        </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function PlanTab({ plan, setPlan, sessions, exercises, setExercises, stretches = STRETCHES, setStretches, recipes = [], mealPlan = [], practice, sila = {}, kitchenRules = [], teachings = [] }) {
  const [showAdd, setShowAdd] = useState(false);
  const [showRaw, setShowRaw] = useState(false);
  const [newEx, setNewEx] = useState({ label: "", unit: "lb", targetSets: 3, targetReps: 12, step: 5, noWeight: false, toChamber: false, sessionType: "both" });
  const [generating, setGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [regenKey, setRegenKey] = useState(null);
  const [expandedKey, setExpandedKey] = useState(null);
  const [importStatus, setImportStatus] = useState("");
  const fileInputRef = React.useRef(null);

  const exportData = () => {
    const payload = { sessions, plan, exercises, stretches, recipes, mealPlan, practice, sila, kitchenRules, teachings, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `ledger-backup-${localDateStr()}.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const importData = (file) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      try {
        const data = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
        if (data.sessions) lsSet("sessions", JSON.stringify(data.sessions));
        if (data.plan) lsSet("plan", JSON.stringify(data.plan));
        if (data.exercises) lsSet("exercises-config", JSON.stringify(data.exercises));
        if (data.stretches) lsSet("stretches-config", JSON.stringify(data.stretches));
        if (data.recipes) lsSet("recipes-config", JSON.stringify(data.recipes));
        if (data.mealPlan) lsSet("meal-plan", JSON.stringify(data.mealPlan));
        if (data.practice) lsSet("practice-entries", JSON.stringify(data.practice));
        if (data.sila) lsSet("sila", JSON.stringify(data.sila));
        if (data.kitchenRules) lsSet("kitchen-rules", JSON.stringify(data.kitchenRules));
        if (data.teachings) lsSet(TEACHINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(data.teachings));
        setImportStatus("Imported — reloading…");
        setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 800);
      } catch (err) {
        setImportStatus("Couldn't read that file — make sure it's a Ledger backup JSON.");
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  const addExercise = async () => {
    if (!newEx.label.trim()) return;
    const key = newEx.label.trim().replace(/\s+/g, "").replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "");
    setGenerating(true);
    let explainer = "";
    try {
      explainer = await generateExplainer(newEx.label.trim());
    } catch (e) {
      explainer = e.message === "NO_API_KEY" ? "" : "";
    }
    setExercises((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [key]: {
        label: newEx.label.trim(), unit: newEx.unit, targetSets: Number(newEx.targetSets),
        targetReps: Number(newEx.targetReps), step: Number(newEx.step), noWeight: newEx.noWeight,
        explainer, active: !newEx.toChamber, sessionType: newEx.sessionType,
      },
    }));
    setGenerating(false);
    setShowAdd(false);
    setNewEx({ label: "", unit: "lb", targetSets: 3, targetReps: 12, step: 5, noWeight: false, toChamber: false, sessionType: "both" });
  };

  const regenerate = async (key) => {
    setRegenKey(key);
    try {
      const explainer = await generateExplainer(exercises[key].label);
      setExercises((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: { ...prev[key], explainer } }));
    } catch (e) {}
    setRegenKey(null);
  };

  const removeExercise = (key) => {
    setExercises((prev) => {
      const copy = { ...prev };
      delete copy[key];
      return copy;
    });
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>
      <Card title="Backup & transfer" sub="Save your data out, or bring it into a new version of this file">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <button onClick={exportData} style={{ ...btnPrimary, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>Export data</button>
          <button onClick={() => fileInputRef.current && fileInputRef.current.click()} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>Import data</button>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => setShowRaw((v) => !v)} style={{ ...btnGhost, width: "100%", justifyContent: "center", marginTop: 8 }}>
          {showRaw ? "Hide text" : "Show as text"}
        </button>
        {showRaw && (
          <textarea
            readOnly
            onClick={(e) => e.target.select()}
            value={JSON.stringify({ sessions, plan, exercises, stretches, recipes, mealPlan, practice, sila, kitchenRules, teachings, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString() })}
            style={{
              width: "100%", minHeight: 150, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: 10, marginTop: 8,
              fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, lineHeight: 1.5,
              background: "#E7E0D0", color: "#1B1B1B", resize: "vertical",
            }}
          />
        )}
        <input ref={fileInputRef} type="file" accept="application/json" style={{ display: "none" }}
          onChange={(e) => { if (e.target.files[0]) importData(e.target.files[0]); }} />
        {importStatus && <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 6 }}>{importStatus}</div>}
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#6E6759", marginTop: 8, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
          Whenever you get an updated version of this file from me, export first, open the new file, then import — it carries sessions, plan, exercises, stretches, recipes, meal plan and practice entries. If the download does not start (some phones block it from a local file), use Show as text and copy it into a note instead.
          </div>
      </Card>
      <Card title="Goals">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          {plan.goals.map((g) => (
            <span key={g} style={{
              fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 13,
              padding: "8px 14px", borderRadius: 0, background: "var(--activity)", color: "#F9F6EE",
            }}>{g}</span>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>
      <Card title="Progression rule" sub="How the ladder above updates automatically">
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
          {plan.progressionRule}
        </div>
      </Card>
      <Card title="Recovery" sub="Rest days — checked automatically on the Today tab">
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
          {plan.restGuidance}
        </div>
      </Card>
      <Card title="Stretching &amp; movement prep" sub="How the stretch routine progresses">
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
          {plan.stretchGuidance}
        </div>
      </Card>
      <Card title="Queue" sub="What's next to add or change — items already logged are flagged">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
          {plan.queue.map((q, i) => {
            const alreadyLogged = sessions.some(s => s.exercises && s.exercises[q.key]);
            return (
            <div key={i} style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 10 }}>
              <div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>
                  {exercises[q.key]?.label || q.key}{alreadyLogged && <span style={{ color: "var(--activity)", fontWeight: 500 }}> · already logged</span>}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759" }}>{q.note}</div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => {
                const updated = { ...plan, queue: plan.queue.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i) };
                setPlan(updated);
              }} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", color: "#6E6759" }}>
                <X size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </Card>

      <Card title="Exercises" sub="Add, edit, or remove — new ones get a how-to guide automatically">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
          {Object.entries(exercises).map(([key, cfg]) => (
            <div key={key} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #D8D0BF", paddingBottom: 10 }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
                <button onClick={() => setExpandedKey(expandedKey === key ? null : key)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", textAlign: "left", flex: 1, padding: 0 }}>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 13, color: "#1B1B1B" }}>{cfg.label}</div>
                    {cfg.active === false && (
                      <span style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 10, color: "#1B1B1B", background: "#FBEFCB", padding: "2px 6px", borderRadius: 0 }}>chamber</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "'IBM Plex Mono', monospace", fontSize: 11, color: "#6E6759" }}>
                    {cfg.noWeight ? `${cfg.targetReps} reps/side` : `${cfg.unit} · ${cfg.targetSets}×${cfg.targetReps} · +${cfg.step}${cfg.unit} step`}
                  </div>
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => setExercises((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: { ...prev[key], active: cfg.active === false ? true : false } }))}
                  style={{ ...btnGhost, padding: "6px 10px", fontSize: 11, marginRight: 6 }}>
                  {cfg.active === false ? "Activate" : "To chamber"}
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => removeExercise(key)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", color: "#6E6759" }}>
                  <X size={15} />
                </button>
              </div>
              {expandedKey === key && (
                <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
                  {cfg.explainer ? (
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#1B1B1B", lineHeight: 1.6, background: "#E7E0D0", borderRadius: 0, padding: 10, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }}>
                      {cfg.explainer}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 12, color: "#6E6759", fontStyle: "italic" }}>No how-to guide yet.</div>
                  )}
                  <button onClick={() => regenerate(key)} disabled={regenKey === key} style={{ ...btnGhost, marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12, padding: "8px 12px" }}>
                    {regenKey === key ? "Generating…" : "Regenerate how-to guide"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {showAdd ? (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10, marginTop: 6 }}>
            <input
              value={newEx.label}
              onChange={(e) => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, label: e.target.value }))}
              placeholder="Exercise name (e.g. Cable Fly)"
              style={{ borderRadius: 0, border: "2px solid #1B1B1B", padding: "10px 12px", fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontSize: 13, background: "#F9F6EE", color: "#1B1B1B" }}
            />
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <Chip active={!newEx.noWeight} onClick={() => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, noWeight: false }))}>Weighted</Chip>
              <Chip active={newEx.noWeight} onClick={() => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, noWeight: true }))}>Bodyweight</Chip>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <Chip active={!newEx.toChamber} onClick={() => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, toChamber: false }))}>Add to routine now</Chip>
              <Chip active={newEx.toChamber} onClick={() => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, toChamber: true }))}>Stage in chamber</Chip>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <Chip active={newEx.sessionType === "A"} onClick={() => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, sessionType: "A" }))}>Session A</Chip>
              <Chip active={newEx.sessionType === "B"} onClick={() => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, sessionType: "B" }))}>Session B</Chip>
              <Chip active={newEx.sessionType === "both"} onClick={() => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, sessionType: "both" }))}>Both</Chip>
            </div>
            {!newEx.noWeight && (
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                {["lb", "kg"].map((u) => (
                  <Chip key={u} active={newEx.unit === u} onClick={() => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, unit: u }))}>{u}</Chip>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
            <StepperPair>
              {!newEx.noWeight && <Stepper value={newEx.targetSets} step={1} unit="sets" min={1} onChange={(v) => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, targetSets: v }))} />}
              <Stepper value={newEx.targetReps} step={1} unit="reps" min={1} onChange={(v) => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, targetReps: v }))} />
            </StepperPair>
            {!newEx.noWeight && (
              <Field label="Weight increment when progressing">
                <Stepper value={newEx.step} step={5} unit={newEx.unit} min={0} onChange={(v) => setNewEx((n) => ({ ...n, step: v }))} />
              </Field>
            )}
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <button onClick={() => setShowAdd(false)} style={{ ...btnGhost, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>Cancel</button>
              <button onClick={addExercise} disabled={generating} style={{ ...btnPrimary, flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}>
                {generating ? "Generating how-to…" : "Add exercise"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button onClick={() => setShowAdd(true)} style={{ ...btnGhost, width: "100%", justifyContent: "center", marginTop: 4 }}>
            <Plus size={15} /> Add exercise
          </button>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

applyTheme();

const root = ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root"));
root.render(<App />);
